paḻaṅkaḷaip poṟukkuvatum, kiḻaṅkukaḷai akaḻvatum, tāṉiya vakaikaḷai oḻuṅkāka viḷaivippataṟku vaḻi kāṭṭiṉa. itiliruntu oḻuṅ kāṉa tāvara uṇavu peṟuvataṟku ētuvākiya muṟkāla vēḷāṇmai toṭaṅ kiṟṟu. iṉṟum kīḻnilaiyiluḷḷa camūkaṅkaḷil peṇkaḷ pāṟaiyiṉālum, vaḷaivu taṭiyiṉālum nilattaik kiḷaṟip payiṭukiṉṟārkaḷ. ātikālam mutal peṇkaḷē necavu ceytal, pāy, kūṭai muṭaital mutaliya vēlai kaḷaiyum ceytu vantārkaḷ. kampaḷi koṭukkum vilaṅkukaḷ vaḷarkkap paṭāta kālattil tāvara vakaikaḷiliruntu nār eṭuttu āṭaikaḷ neyyappaṭṭaṉa. cuviṟcarlāntu vāvikaḷil uṟainta makkaḷ (ki.mu.5,000) caṇaliṉālum kampaḷiyiṉālum ceytu uṭutta tuṇikaḷiṉ tuṇṭukaḷ kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. paḻaiya makkaḷuḷ āṇkaḷum peṇkaḷum ōr ōr iṭaṅkaḷil āṭai neytārkaḷ eṉa aṟikiṉṟōm. paṟpala kāraṇaṅkaḷi ṉāl āṭavar peṇkaḷukkuriya vēlaikaḷait tām kaippaṟṟik koṇṭu, avarkaḷ mītu āṇkaḷukkuriyaṉavum, mikak kīḻmaiyāṉavum uḷḷa vēlaikaḷaic cumattiṉārkaḷ eṉat terikiṉṟatu. jermaṉiyil, pāṉai caṭṭi vaṉaivōrum āṭai neyvōrum mattiya kālam varai yil tāḻntavarkaḷākak koḷḷappaṭṭaṉar. itaṟkuk kāraṇam at toḻilkaḷ peṇkaḷukku uriyaṉavāyiruntaṉavēyākum. taiyal vēlai ceyvōr peṇ taṉmaiyuḷḷavarkaḷākavum, parikacikkattakkavar kaḷākavum nīṇṭa kālam karutappaṭalāyiṉar.
tāy atikārattiṉ toṭakkam
muṟkāla maṉitaṉ āṇ peṇ cērkkaiyiṉāl kuḻantai piṟakkiṉṟatu eṉa aṟiyat toṭaṅkiya piṉ tāyāṭci muṟaiyāṉa camūka vaḷarcci uṇṭāyiṟṟu. muṟkālattil kūṭṭu maṇaṅkaḷ camūkaṅkaḷukkuḷ iruntaṉa. itaṉāl tantaikkum piḷḷaikkum uṟavu uṇṭākavillai. tāykkum piḷḷaikaḷukkum māttiram uṇmaiyāṉa iratta uṟavu uṇṭāki yiruntatu. tāy kuḻantaikku nīṇṭakālam pāl ūṭṭi vaḷarttāḷ. ataṉāl avaḷukkum piḷḷaikkum nīṇṭa toṭarpu uṇṭāyiruntatu. nākarika maṭainta makkaḷiṭaiyē uḷḷa tāyilum pārkkap piṟpōkkāṉa makka ḷiṭaiyē uḷḷa tāy, taṉ kuḻantaikku nīṇṭa kālam pāl ūṭṭi vaḷark kiṟāḷ. cātāraṇa kālam iraṇṭu allatu mūṉṟu āṇṭukaḷ, āstirēliya tāy āṟu āṇṭukaḷ kuḻantaikkup pālūṭṭi vaḷarkkiṟāḷ. vaṭa amerikka peṇkaḷ paṉṉiraṇṭu āṇṭukaḷ varaiyum kuḻantaikkup pāl koṭukkiṟārkaḷ.
ivvāṟu tāykkum kuḻantaikkum uṇṭāṉa toṭarpiṉāl tāyaṉpu atikam vaḷarntatu. ivvāṟu amainta camūkattil tantai aṉṉiya ṉākak karutappaṭṭāṉ; iratta uṟavu uṭaiyavaṉākak karutappaṭavillai. peṇṇukku atikāram uṇṭāyiṟṟu. vaḷarnta putalvar kuṭumpattaip pātukāppōrākaviruntaṉar; putalviyar peṇkaḷukkuriya kaṭamaikaḷil utavi purivōrāyiruntaṉar.
in nilaimaiyiṉāl tōṉṟiya tāyāṭciyē āstirēliyā, āppirikkā, amerikkā, āciyā mutaliya nāṭukaḷiṉ paḻaṅkuṭi kaḷiṭaiyē nilaittuḷḷaṉa. paḻaiya nākarika makkaḷiṭaiyē tāyāṭci muṟai iruntatu. pāpilōṉiya āvaṇaṅkaḷil “tāyum tantaiyum”, “maṉaiviyum kaṇavaṉum” eṉpaṉa pōlap peṇṇiṉ peyarē mutaliṟ kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. maṉitaṉ nākarikamaṭaintirunta kālattilum tāyāṭci muṟai acaikka muṭiyāta paṭi valimaiyaṭaintiruntateṉat terikiṉṟatu. pāpilōṉiyap peṇkaḷ āṇkaḷai otta urimai peṟṟiruntārkaḷ eṉpataip paṭṭaiyaṅkaḷ vaḻiyāka aṟikiṉṟōm. hamurāpi kālattil (ki.mu.2,250) peṇkaḷ kōvilkaḷil kurumārākaviruntārkaḷ. āvaṇaṅkaḷil peṇkaḷ cāṭcikaḷākavum kaiyeḻuttiṭṭārkaḷ.
ekiptiya āvaṇaṅkaḷil iṉṉa tāyiṉ makaṉ eṉṟē perum pālum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. tantaiyiṉ peyar arumaiyāka ōrōriṭattiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. kirēkkar ekiptai veṟṟi koṇṭa kālattil ekiptiyar tāy vaḻiyālēyē aṟiyappaṭṭārkaḷ. piṉpu ciṟitu ciṟiyatākat tāyiṉ peyarukkup patil tantaiyiṉ peyar iṭam peṟṟatu. paḻaiya ōviya eḻuttukkaḷil tāy, vīṭṭukkāri eṉak kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷāḷ.” arumai yāṉa talaivi” allatu “uṉatu kaṇavaṉiṉ aṉpukkuriya illāḷ” eṉṟu kaṇavaṉ avaḷai aḻaittāṉ. “ekiptil peṇkaḷ kaṭaitteru vukkup pōy koḷvaṉavu viṟpaṉavukaḷaic ceykiṟārkaḷ. āṇkaḷ vīṭṭiliruntu tuṇi neykiṟārkaḷ. putalvarukkup peṟṟōrai ātarikka vēṇṭiya kaṭamai illai; putalvikkē ak kaṭamai uṇṭu” eṉa eratōtacu eṉṉum paḻaiya kirēkka varalāṟṟāciriyar kūṟiyuḷḷār. ēratōtacukku (ki.mu. 480) nāṉūṟu āṇṭukaḷ piṉ vāḻnta tayatōras ik kūṟṟiṉai valiyuṟutti, aracaṉilum pārkka aracikkuk kūṭiya atikāram uṇṭu eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. tirumaṇa uṭaṉpaṭikkaiyil kaṇavaṉ maṉaivikkuk kīḻppaṭivatāka vākkaḷittāṉ eṉṟum avar kuṟippiṭṭār. ekiptiya peṇkaḷiṉ cottu avarkaḷ atikārattukkuṭ paṭṭu iruntatu. avarkaḷ ataṉait tamatu eṇṇappaṭi evvāṟu vēṇṭiṉum ceytu koḷḷalām.
ekiptilē aracar mutal ellā makkaḷiṭaiyum cakōtaraṉ cakōtariyai maṇakkum vaḻakkam tōṉṟi vaḷarntiruntatu. iraṇṭām tālami (ki.mu.285-247) eṉṉum ekiptiya aracaṉ pilāṭalpōs eṉat taṉakku maṟu peyar vaittuk koṇṭāṉ. pilāṭalpōs eṉpataṟku cakōtariyaik kātalikkiṉṟavaṉ eṉpatu poruḷ. paḻaiya vaḻakkattaip piṉpaṟṟi avaṉ ārciṉō eṉṉum taṉatu cakōtariyai maṇantāṉ. ekiptu pala ciṟṟaracar nāṭukaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭiruntatu. ovvoru ciṟiya nāṭṭaiyum ovvoru aracaṉ āṇṭāṉ. avvaracaṉukku āṭci yurimai tantai vaḻiyālaṉṟit tāy vaḻiyākavē vantatu. atu iṉṟu tiruvitāṅkūril naṭaimuṟaiyiluḷḷa marumakkaḷ tāyattai ottatu. etiyōppiyariṉ aracurimai aracaṉiṉ piḷḷaikaḷai aṭaiyāmal avaṉuṭaiya cakōtariyiṉ piḷḷaikaḷai aṭaintatu eṉṟu nikkōlas eṉṉum paḻaiya varalāṟṟāciriyar kūṟiyuḷḷār.
carittirakālam varaiyil tāyāṭci emmaṭṭum camūkaṅkaḷil valimai peṟṟiruntateṉpatai viḷakkuvataṟkut tāyāṭciyaik kuṟittuk kūṟiyuḷḷōm. varavara atikārattāl vaḷarntu vanta āṭavar peṇkaḷai niyāyamāka naṭattat tavaṟiṉārkaḷ eṉak kūṟamuṭiyātu.
āṭavaṉ pala peṇkaḷai maṇattal
ekiptilē celvantaṉāyuḷḷavaṉ tāṉ virumpiya attaṉai peṇkaḷaiyum vaippāṭṭikaḷāka vaittiruntāṉ. āṉāl avaṉuṭaiya muṟaiyāṉa maṉaiviyē vīṭṭiṉ talaivi eṉappaṭṭāḷ. araṇmaṉai kaḷil ‘peṇkaḷiṉ vīṭu’ eṉappaṭṭa taṉimaiyāṉa oru iṭam iruntatu. paipilas ēṭu oṉṟil, vaippāṭṭikaḷiṉ mēṟkoṇṭa kātalāl taṉ mītu paṟṟu oḻinta aracaṉaip paṟṟik kūṟiya irāṇi yoruttiyiṉ vākkiyaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
viyapicārak kuṟṟamum taṇṭaṉaiyum
peṇṇai vilaikku vāṅki allatu tiruṭic contamākkiya pōtum viyapicāram maṉitaṉukkuk kauravak kuṟaivāṉatu eṉṟu koḷḷap paṭṭatu. ellā nāṭukaḷilum kīḻ nilaiyiluḷḷa makkaḷiṭaiyē peṇkaḷ viyapicārattiṟkut taṇṭikkappaṭukiṟārkaḷ; āṇ ciṟitēṉum taṇṭaṉai aṭaivatillai. peṇṇai avaḷuṭaiya peṟṟōriṭam pōkki avarkaḷiṭamiruntu peṇṇukku vilaiyākak koṭutta poruḷaip peṟṟuk koḷḷutal, peṇṇiṉ avayavaṅkaḷai veṭṭutal, allatu avaḷaik kolai ceytal, pōlvaṉa viyapicārak kuṟṟattukkup peṇ aṭainta taṇṭaṉaikaḷākum. cila camayaṅkaḷil viyapicārak kuṟṟañ cāṭṭappaṭṭavaṉ naṭṭa īṭu koṭukka vēṇṭiyiruntatu; cila camayam kolait taṇṭaṉaiyum peṟṟāṉ. ekiptil viyapicārak kuṟṟattukkuṭ paṭṭa peṇkaḷiṉ mūkku aṟukkappaṭṭatu. apāci (Apache) eṉṉum amerikkariṭaiyē ivvaḻakku iṉṟum naṭaipeṟukiṉṟatu. mūkku aṟukkappaṭāviṭṭāl avaḷ uyirōṭu putaikkappaṭṭāḷ. kuṟṟattuk kuḷḷāṉavaṉ aṭikkappaṭṭāṉ.
peṇkaḷ āṇkaḷaik kavarutaṟkut tammai alaṅkarittal
ekiptiya peṇkaḷ āṭavarai vacīkarappaṭuttuvataṟkut tammai evvaḷavu alaṅkarikka muṭiyumō avvaḷavukku alaṅkarittuk koṇṭārkaḷ. avarkaḷ palavakai mēṉimiṉukkup poruḷkaḷ, maṇap poruḷkaḷ, cāyaṅkaḷ, uṭai alaṅkāraṅkaḷ pōṉṟavaikaḷaip payaṉ paṭuttiṉar. tāyāṭci valiyaṭaintiruntapōtum āṭavaṉ evvāṟu camūkattil taṉṉai uyarvāka vaikka muṭintatu eṉpatai ivai viḷakkukiṉṟaṉa. patāhōtep (Path-hotep ki.mu.3,350) eṉṉum aracaṉ peṇkaḷai āṭavarukkup piṉvarumāṟu puttimati kūṟiyuḷḷāṉ. “nī puttimāṉāyiṉ uṉatu maṉaiviyiṭam aṉpāyiru; avaḷukku nalla uṇavu koṭu; avaḷai aḻakāka uṭukkum paṭi cey; avaḷukku maṇap poruḷkaḷai vāṅkik koṭu. iṉṉum avaḷukku makiḻcci koṭukkak kūṭiyavaikaḷai nī irukkumaṭṭum cey. avaḷ taṉṉaiyuṭaiyavaṉiṉ takutikkēṟṟavaḷāy vāḻa vēṇṭum. nī avaḷiṭam koṭumaiyāka naṭavātē. muraṭṭuttaṉamāṉa atikārattilum pārkka aṉpu naṉmaikaḷaic ceykiṉṟatu. mukakkaṇṇāṭiyil taṉṉaip pārkkumpōtu avaḷ makiḻcciyaṭaivāḷ. avaḷ makiḻcciyōṭu uṉatu vīṭṭiliruntu vīṭṭu vēlaikaḷai virumpic ceyvāḷ.” itaṉāl, peṇṇiṉ atikārattuk kuṭaiya tāyāṭci nīṇṭa kālam naṭaipeṟṟu vantapōtum atu kālattil kuṟaiyat toṭaṅki viṭṭateṉat terikiṉṟatu.

yūtariṭaiyē tāyāṭci muṟai
kiṟittuva vēta kālattil yūtariṭaiyē tāyāṭci muṟai iruntateṉak kāṇkiṉṟōm. kiṟittuva vētattil aracaraip paṟṟiya puttakattil aracaṉiṉ peyarōṭu tāyiṉ peyar iṇaikkappaṭṭiruk kiṉṟatu. araca capaiyil aracaṉiṉ tāykku mutaṉmai aḷikkappaṭṭatu. “ākavē oruvaṉ taṉatu tāyaiyum tantaiyaiyum viṭṭut taṉatu maṉaiviyōṭu cērntu iruvarum oruvarāvārkaḷā?” (āti ākamam 11-24) eṉak kiṟittuva vētattiṉ pala viṭaṅkaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. iti ṉuṭaiya poruḷ, oruttaṉ oru peṇṇai maṇantu tāyāṭci vaḻakkap paṭi avaḷ kuṭumpattaic cērntavaṉāvāṉ eṉpatē.

pulveḷikaḷil vāḻnta epirēya makkaḷiṭaiyē tāyāṭci muṉṉamē maṟaintu pōyiṟṟu. āṭu māṭu vaḷarttu alaintu tiriyum makkaḷiṉ vāḻkkaikkum payiriṭṭu ōr iṭattil taṅkiyirukkum makkaḷiṉ vāḻkkaikkum vēṟupāṭu uṇṭu.
tantaiyiṉ atikāram
vēṭṭaiyāṭum, āṭavarē toṭakkattil vilaṅkukaḷaip piṭittup paḻakki vaḷarttārkaḷ. pulveḷikaḷil mēyntu maṉitariṉ kavaṉippukkuṭpaṭātu perukum āṭumāṭukaḷiṉ kūṭṭaṅkaḷukkum āṭavarē talaivarākaviruntaṉar. mantaikaḷ pāl, iṟaicci mutaliya uṇavukaḷaiyum, tōl, kampaḷi mutaliya uṭaikaḷaiyum koṭuttaṉa. itaṉāl avai mikavum matippukkuriya cottākapperukiṉa. āṭṭu mantai, māṭṭu mantaikaḷai atikamāka vaittiruppatāl avaikaḷiṉ contakkāraṉ putiya mēyccal nilattait tēṭa vēṇṭiyiruntatu. ākavē avaṉ taṉatu uṟaiviṭattai aṭikkaṭi māṟṟa vēṇṭiyiruntatu. periya āṭu māṭukaḷiṉ kūṭṭaṅkaḷaip pātukāppataṟku avaṉukku vēṭṭai yāṭutal, pōr ceytalkaḷiṟ payiṟciyuḷḷa utaviyāḷar tēvaippaṭṭaṉar. ākavē avaṉ aṭimaikaḷaip peṟum avaciyam uṇṭāyiṟṟu. aṭimai kaḷaip peṟṟatum avaṉ taṉatu putalvarkaḷukku mantai mēykkum payiṟciyaḷittu mantaiyai avarkaḷ kavaṉikkumpaṭi ceytāṉ. āppirikkāvilē āṭumāṭukaḷai vaḷarkkum kūṭṭattiṉariṭaiyē avai viṭappaṭṭirukkum paṭṭikkuḷ peṇkaḷ cella aṉumatikkap paṭamāṭṭārkaḷ. avaikaḷai mēyppatu, pālkaṟappatu ellām āṭavarāl ceyyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

peṇkaḷukkum āṇkaḷukkumuḷḷa camūkat toṭarpai poruḷā tārat toṭarpāṉa māṟṟaṅkaḷum vēṟupaṭuttiyuḷḷaṉa. tāy vaḻiyiliruntu urimai uṇṭāvataṟkup patil tantai āṭci uṇṭāyiṟṟu. mantaik kuriyavaṉē taṉṉai uṇavukku etirpārkkum kuṭumpattiṉ maṟṟaiyavar kaḷukkellām talaivaṉāṉāṉ. avaṉukkut tuṇai purivataṟkup pala āṭkaḷiṉ utavi vēṇṭiyiruntatu. ākavē avaṉukkup pala kuḻantai kaḷaip peṟuvataṟkup pala peṇkaḷ tēvaippaṭṭārkaḷ.

pāpilōṉilum ekiptilum peṇkaḷ campantamāṉa māṟṟam uṇṭākat toṭaṅkiyiruntaṉa. piṟkālattil ōriṭattil taṅkiyirunta epirēyariṭaiyē mēlum tāyāṭci viḻukaiyaṭaivatāyiṟṟu. kiṟittuva maṟaiyiṉ paḻaiya ēṟpāṭṭil āṇ kuḻantai piṟantāl yēkōvāk kaṭavuḷukku naṉṟi celuttuvatu pōlap peṇ kuḻantai piṟantāl naṉṟi celuttum vākkiyaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭavillai. peṇkaḷ peṟṟōr kaḷuṭaiya cottāyiruntatu piṉpu kaṇavaṉiṉ cottu āṉārkaḷ. peṇ vilai matippuḷḷa vēlaiyāḷāyiruntāḷ. avaḷ mikak kaṭumaiyāṉa vīṭṭu vēlaikaḷaic ceytāḷ. avaḷ aṭimaipōl vēlai ceytatu māttiramallāmal avaḷiṭam tāṉ urimai pārāṭṭakkūṭiya cottu etuvum irukkavillai. pāpilōṉiyarāl ciṟai piṭikkappaṭum kālam varaiyil āṇkaḷ pala peṇkaḷai maṇantārkaḷ. āṭavaṉ peṇṇai vilai koṭuttu vāṅkiṉāṉ, cottu āṇ vaḻiyaic cērntatu. āṇ cantati illāviṭil cottup peṇṇaic cērntatu; āṉāl avaḷ ak kuṭumpattil māttiram kaṇavaṉait terintu koḷḷutal vēṇṭum, peṇṇiṉ kaṭamai piḷḷaikaḷaip peṟuvatum vīṭṭai oḻuṅkāka vaittuk koḷḷuvatumē eṉṟu karutappaṭṭatu.

ilakkiya kāla makkaḷai nām nōkkumpōtu eratōtacu kūṟi yirukkum oru nikaḻcci itaṟku māṟākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. ciṉṉa āciyā viṉ teṉmēṟku mūlaiyiṟ kuṭiyēṟiyiruntavarkaḷum, kirēttā (Crete) viṉiṉṟu vantavarkaḷ eṉappaṭṭavarkaḷumākiya ilaiciyariṭaiyē piḷḷaikaḷ tāy vaḻiyāl aṟiyappaṭṭaṉar. oruvaṉai nōkki avaṉ kuṭumpa vaḻiyai viṉāviṉāl avaṉ taṉatu tāy allatu tāyiṉtāy peyaraik kūṟuvāṉ. peṇ, aṭimai oruvaṉaic cērntu piḷḷaiyaip peṟṟāl piḷḷai muṟaimaiyāṉatākak koḷḷappaṭṭatu. āṇ, aṭimaip peṇṇaic cērvatāl piṟanta piḷḷaikaḷ aṭimaikaḷākavum muṟaikēṭākap piṟantaṉavākavum koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. cottu tāyiṭa miruntu makaḷai aṭaintatu; makaṉai aṭaiyavillai.

kirēkka nākarikattiṉ muṉmuṟaiyil nām tāyāṭciyaik kāṇ kiṉṟōm. ivvaṭaiyāḷaṅkaḷ paḻaṅkataikaḷilēyē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. atēṉiya caṭṭam tantai vaḻiyiluḷḷa (tantaiyiṉ cakōtaraṉiṉ peṇ ṇākiya) cakōtariyai maṇakka aṉumatikkiṉṟatu; āṉāl tāy vaḻiyil ivvāṟu maṇakkum paṭi aṉumatikkavillai. paḻaiya kirēkkariṭattil kātal eṉpatu iṉṟaiya kīḻ nilaiyiluḷḷa makkaḷiṭaiyē kāṇappaṭum kātalaip paṟṟiya karuttaiviṭa vēṟāka irukkavillai. kaṉṉip peṇkaḷ peṇkaḷukku viṭappaṭṭuḷḷa aṟaikaḷiliruntu vaḷarn tārkaḷ, peṇ maṇapparuvam aṭaintatum peṟṟōr maṇamakaṉait tēṭi avaṉiṭattil avaḷaik koṭuttārkaḷ. avaḷ kaṇavaṉiṉ vīṭṭukkuc ceṉṟu aṅkup peṇkaḷ irukkum aṟaiyil vāḻntāḷ. avaḷ kalvi yaṟivillātiruntāḷ; avaḷukkup peṇkaḷukkuriya taiyal vēlai māttiram teriyum. avaḷ veḷiyē cellavum āṭavarōṭu paḻakavum maṉa vaḷarccikkuriyavaikaḷai aṟiyavum muṭiyāmal iruntāḷ.

piṟkālattil āṭci muṟaikaḷil māṟṟam uṇṭāṉa pōtu kirēkkap peṇkaḷiṉ nilai iṉṉum mōcamaṭaintatu. maṉaiviyiruk kum aṟaiyaik kāval kāppataṟku aṭimaip peṇ orutti niyamikkap paṭṭāḷ. maṉaiviyirukkum aṟaiyai aṭaittut tāḷ iṭṭu avaḷaik kāval kāppatē nalla muṟai eṉṟu kaṇavaṉ karutiṉāṉ. paḻaiya tattuva cāttirikaḷ, peṇkaḷ āṇkaḷukkuc camamāṉavarkaḷallar eṉak karuti ṉārkaḷ. ākavē caṭṭattil peṇkaḷukku vāyppāṉavai evaiyum irukkavillai. kirīcilē spāṭṭiya, ṭōriyap peṇkaḷ viṭutalai yaṭaintiruntārkaḷ eṉac cilar kūṟuvatuṇṭu. ik karuttut tavaṟuṭaiyatu. spāṭṭiya, ṭōriyap peṇkaḷ veḷiyē ceṉṟu viḷaiyāṭṭukaḷilum malyuttaṅkaḷilum kalantu koḷḷumpaṭiyum viṭappaṭṭārkaḷ. itu avarkaḷ uṭal nalam peṟṟu nalla kuḻantaikaḷaip peṟum poruṭṭum, āṭavariṉ naṉmaiyaik karutiyum aṉumatikkappaṭṭa vaḻakkamēyākum.

ak kālattil kirīcil iṉṉoru vakaiyākap peṇkaḷ muṉṉukku vantārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ parattaiyarāvar (Hetairai). ivarkaḷ āṭavariṉ kavarccikkuṭaiyavarkaḷāyiruntaṉar. vīṭṭu maṉaiviyarkaḷilum pārkka ivarkaḷukku ulaka naṭaiyum, kalviyum, āṭavarai vaciyap paṭuttum taṉmaiyum, nalla paḻakkaṅkaḷum teriyum. kirēkkak kaṇavarkaḷ ivarkaḷaiyē cuṟṟit tirintaṉar. cākirāṭs, arisṭōṭṭil mutaliya tattuva ñāṉikaḷum ivarkaḷiṉ kavarccikkuṭpaṭṭiruntaṉar. vīṭṭukkāri eṉappaṭṭa peṇ ivvāṟu parattaiyaic cuṟṟittiriyum kaṇavaṉaik kaliyāṇa nīkkam ceytu koḷḷalām. ap parattaiyai vīṭṭukkuk koṇṭu varāta aḷavil avaḷ avvāṟu ceytal muṭiyātu. perikkiḷis (Pericles) ivvāṟu ceytamaiyāl kaliyāṇa nīkkam ceyyappaṭṭār. avar aimpatu vayatuṭaiyavarāyum, vaḷarnta makkaḷai uṭaiyavarāyum iruntapōtum aspēciyā (Aspesia) eṉṉum parattaiyai maṇantār. ṭemostīṉis eṉṉum kirēkkar kūṟiyiruppatu varumāṟu: “nāṅkaḷ eṅkaḷuṭaiya tēvaikaḷukkum ūḻiyattukkumāka vaippāṭṭikaḷai (aṭimaikaḷai) vaittirukkiṟōm; eṅkaḷ kātalukkum iṉpattukku mākap parattaiyar irukkiṟārkaḷ; vīṭṭaik kavaṉikka maṉaiviyar irukkiṟārkaḷ.”
1parattaimai
muṟaimaiyāṉa toṭarpu illāta āṭavarōṭu cērkkai vait tirukkum peṇ parattai eṉappaṭuvāḷ. āṭavaṉiṉ iyalpāṉa taṉmai oru peṇṇai maṇappataṉṟu. tirumaṇaṅkaḷ oḻukka campantattilum pārkkap poruḷātāra campantamuṭaiyaṉa. muṟkālattil palavāṉ allatu celvavāṉ, tāṉ virumpiya attaṉai peṇkaḷaik koḷḷaiyiṉāl akappaṭutti allatu vilai koṭuttu vāṅki vaittirukkalām. kīḻ nilaiyiluḷḷa makkaḷiṭaiyē iṉṟum immuṟai kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. kiṟittuva vētattiṉ paḻaiya ēṟpāṭṭil kūṟappaṭum cālamaṉ aracaṉiṭat til maṉaiviyarum vaippāṭṭikaḷumāka āyiram peṇkaḷ varaiyilirun tārkaḷ. palamillātavarum, vaṟiyavarum oru maṉaiviyai vaittiruntaṉar, allatu maṉaivi illātu iruntaṉar. poruḷ ātāra nilaimai naṉṟā yillāviṭiṉum peṇtuṇai vēṇṭumeṉṉum viruppiṉāl āṭavaṉ oruttiyai vilai koṭuttu vāṅkuvāṉ allatu tiruṭik koṇṭu varuvāṉ. ivvaṭippaṭaiyiliruntē oḻukkam oḻukkamiṉmai eṉṉum niyāyaṅkaḷ tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. oḻukkam eṉpatu cātāraṇamāka makka ḷiṭaiyē naṭappatu; oḻukkamiṉmai am makkaḷiṭaiyē naṭavātatu. piṉpu caṭṭaṅkaḷ tōṉṟi oḻukkamiṉmaikkut taṇṭaṉaikaḷai vitikkiṉṟaṉa. peṇkaḷum āṇkaḷum vāḻkiṉṟa iṭaṅkaḷil ic caṭṭaṅkaḷ nilai peṟukiṉṟaṉa.

paḻaiya caṭṭaṅkaḷai vakutta cōlaṉ1 (ki.mu. 504) eṉṉum kirēkkar viyapicārattai caṭṭatiṭṭappaṭutta niṉaittu aracāṅka viyapicāra viṭuti oṉṟait tiṟantāṉ. av viṭutikku oṉṟaraip ‘peṉs’ (oṉṟarai aṇā) varaiyil kaṭṭaṇam koṭukkum āṭavaṉ aṉumatikkappaṭṭāṉ. ivvāṟu kiṭaitta poruḷaik koṇṭu cōlaṉ apirōṭaiṟ eṉṉum kātalukkuriya peṇ teyvattiṟkuk kōvil kaṭṭiṉāṉ. itu piṟkālat til yūta kurumār erucalēm2 kōvilaikkaṭṭuvataṟku oḻukkantavaṟiya peṇkaḷiṭamiruntu vari taṇṭiyatu pōṉṟatākum, parattaiyarākiya ik kīḻttarap peṇkaḷ maṇavāḻkkaikkup peruntīṅku viḷaivittārkaḷ. kirēkkar āciya makkaḷiṉ ullāca vāḻkkaiyai aṟintu pakaṭṭāṉa parattaiyariṉ paḻakkattaip peṟṟārkaḷ. muṉ kūṟappaṭṭa aspēciyā eṉṉum perikkiḷiciṉ maṉaivi atēṉcil naṭattiya parattaiyar paḷḷik kūṭattukku atēṉiya makkaḷiṉ peṇkaḷum, putalviyarum ceṉṟaṉar. pairaṉi (Pyryne) eṉṉum parattai taṉatu uḻaippil oru pakutiyai tīps nakariṉ aḻinta matilait tiruttuvataṟkuk koṭuttāḷ. cākiraṭīs, arisṭāṭil, piḷāṭō mutaliyōrukkup parattaiyariṉ uṟavu iṉṟi yamaiyātiruntatu. parattaiyar tāymārum, maṉaiviyarumāyumiruntaṉar. parattaiyar peṟum piḷḷaikaḷ muṟaiyāṉa kuḻantaikaḷākak koḷḷap paṭṭamaiyāl iḷam peṇkaḷ parattaimait toḻilaik kaikkoḷḷum paṭi tūṇṭappaṭṭārkaḷ.

ivvāṟu airōppāvil aḻakuk kalaikkalviyum parattaiyariṭa miruntu ārampittuḷḷatu. parattaiyariṉ iyakkamē peṇkaḷiṉ mutal iyakkam eṉak kūṟalām. piṟkāla cīrtirutta vaḷarccik kālaṅkaḷil peṇkaḷ im muṟaiyaic ciṟitu māṟṟi āṭavar viṭuvittuk koḷḷa muṭiyātapaṭi avarkaḷukkup putiya vilaṅku iṭṭārkaḷ.
urōmap peṇkaḷ

urōmap peṇkaḷiṉ nilai, pārvaikkuk kirēkkap peṇkaḷiṉ nilaiyilum uyarntateṉat tōṉṟum. urōmarkaḷiṭaiyum āṭavar kaṭṭuppaṭuttappaṭāta talaivarkaḷāyiruntaṉar. maṇac caṭaṅkil peṇṇiṉ kaiyil tiṟavukōl koṭukkappaṭṭatu. itu vīṭṭu atikāram avaḷiṭam aḷikkappaṭṭataik kuṟikkiṉṟatu. maṇamakaṉ taṉatu kaiviralil irumpu mōtirattai aṇintāṉ. itu avaṉ kaṭṭuppaṭuttappaṭṭatai aṟivikkum. peṇṇukkuc ciṟuvayatil maṇam niccayikkappaṭṭatu; paṉṉiraṇṭu allatu patiṉmūṉṟu vayatil maṇam nikaḻntatu. peṇ kaṇavaṉiṉ tantai yāl terintu eṭukkappaṭṭāḷ. avaḷ tirumaṇattukkut taṉatu cammatam koṭukka vēṇṭiyatillai; tantaiyiṉ pātukāppiliruntu avaḷ kaṇavaṉ pātukāppukkuc ceṉṟāḷ. kirēkka maṉaivikkum urōmaṉ maṉaivikkumiṭaiyil camūka nilaimaiyil vēṟupāṭu uṇṭu. urōmaṉ peṇ, peṇkaḷukku eṉat taṉimaiyākak kaṭṭappaṭṭa iṭattil vāḻa villai. avaḷ kaṇavaṉōṭu iruntu uṇṭāḷ; viruntukaḷil kalantu koṇṭāḷ. avaḷ vīṭṭukku varupavarkaḷai etirkoṇṭāḷ; virumpiya pōtu vīṭṭai viṭṭu veḷiyē ceṉṟāḷ; niyāya maṉṟaṅkaḷil vaḻak kāḷiyāka allatu cāṭciyākac ceṉṟāḷ. kaṇavaṉ vīṭṭikkut talaiva ṉākaviruntāṉ. veḷiyiluḷḷa vēlaikaḷ ellāvaṟṟukkum avaṉē poṟuppuṭaiyavaṉ. kuṭumpattiluḷḷavarkaḷ aṭimaikaḷ vilaṅkukaḷ ellām talaivaṉukkuriyavai. mika muṟkālattil maṉaivi matuvaruntum paḻakkamuḷḷavaḷāyiruntāl, avaḷaik kaṇavaṉ koṉṟuviṭutal kuṟṟamākak karutappaṭavillai. ṭomiṭṭiyas eṉpavaṉ peṇ orutti vaittiyaṉ coṉṉa aḷavukku atikam matu aruntiyataṟkāka avaḷ taṉ kaliyāṇattāl kiṭaitta paṇattai iḻantupōkumpaṭi tīrppuc ceytāṉ. makkaḷiṉ āṭci uṇṭāṉapōtu urōm nāṭṭil tantai āṭci naṭaipeṟṟatu. peṇkaḷ urimaiyaip paṟṟiyirunta paḻaiya kālak karuttukkaḷ maṟaintapōtu avarkaḷ taṅkaḷukku vāyppāka avaikaḷai māṟṟikkoṇṭārkaḷ. vāḻkkaiyiṉ ellāt tuṟaikaḷilum avarkaḷukkum paṅku iruntatu. ippoḻutu avarkaḷ tamatu uṭaimai kaḷaiyum celvattaiyum tamatu atikārattilēyē vaittiruntārkaḷ. peṇkaḷ campantamāṉa karumaṅkaḷaip paṟṟi ālōcippataṟku ivarkaḷ aracāṅkattilum vēṇṭappaṭṭārkaḷ.

“ellāc cātiyiṉarum taṅkaḷ peṇkaḷaik kaṭṭiyāḷkiṟārkaḷ; nāṅkaḷ āṇkaḷai māttiram āḷkiṉṟōm; eṅkaḷukkum mēlākap peṇkaḷ āḷkiṟārkaḷ. nām namatu peṇṇai aṭakki āḷum tiṟamaiyuḷḷavarkaḷāyiruntāl peṇkaḷ campantamāṉa tollaikaḷ irā. nām vīṭṭuk karumaṅkaḷil atikārattaiyiḻantuviṭṭapaṭiyāl potuk karumaṅkaḷilum peṇkaḷ eṅkaḷaik kaṭṭiyāḷukiṉṟārkaḷ. nāṅkaḷ taṉippaṭṭu niṉṟu avarkaḷai etirkkamuṭiyātu. avarkaḷiṉ oṉṟu cērnta atikārattai nām añcukiṉṟōm. inta oḻuṅkaṟṟavarkaḷiṭattil eṅkaḷai nām oppuvittāl avarkaḷiṉ pērācaiyāṉa ceyalkaḷukku muṭivu kāṇa iyalātu. uṇmaiyākak kūṟumiṭattu peṇkaḷukku viṭutalaiyē kūṭātu. avarkaḷukku ellāc ceyalkaḷilum kaṭṭuppāṭu vēṇṭum. peṇ eṅkaḷōṭu cama urimaiyaip peṟṟāl avaḷ eṅkaḷai viraivil piṉṉāl taḷḷiviṭuvāḷ” eṉṟu kēṭō (Cato- ki.mu. 195) kūṟiyuḷḷār.

kirēkka nākarikam paraviyatālum, aracāṅkattil atikāram uṇ ṭāṉamaiyiṉālum, celvam atikarittamaiyiṉālum urōmap peṇkaḷ kēṭō kūṟiyatupōla āṇkaḷiṉ kaṭṭuppāṭṭiṉiṉṟum viṭupaṭṭu viyakkattakka muṉṉēṟṟamaṭaintārkaḷ, kirēkka nāṭṭil kāṇappaṭṭa parattaiyariṉ naṭaimuṟaikaḷ periya aḷaviṉatāyum kīḻāṉa taṉmai yuṭaiyatāyum urōmil tōṉṟalāyiṉa. periya aracāṅka uttiyōkat tariṉ maṉaiviyar, taṇṭaṉai peṟātu tamatu aḷavu kaṭanta iccaiyait taṇittuk koḷḷum poruṭṭut tammaip pakiraṅka vilai mātarākap pativu ceytu koṇṭārkaḷ. ak kālattil caṭṭam viyapicārak kuṟṟaṅ kaḷukkuk kaṭuntaṇṭaṉai vitittatu. tēva arampaiyai otta kaṭṭaḻaki yākiya mecaliṉiyā (Messalinia) pōṉṟa irāṇikaḷē viyapicāra viṭuti kaḷil parattamait toḻilai naṭattiṉārkaḷ. peṇkaḷiṉ viṭutalai evvaḷavu tūram ceṉṟateṉpatai nām aṟintu koḷḷalām.

urōmap peṇkaḷiṉ viṭutalaiyiṉāl piṟappu vikitam kuṟaintatu. itaṉaic caṭṭaṅkaḷiṉāl uyar nilaikkuk koṇṭu vara muṭiyavillai. itu urōmariṉ viḻukaikku oru kāraṇamākaviruntatu.

peṇ pala kaṇavarai maṇattal
urōmar piriṭṭaṉmītu paṭaiyeṭutta kālattil pirittaṉil peṇkaḷ pala kaṇavarai orē kālattil maṇantiruntārkaḷ. itaṉaic cīcar ‘ṭi pellō kalikkō’ eṉṉum taṉatu nūliṟ kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. oru peṇ cātāraṇamāka pattu mutal paṉṉiraṇṭu kaṇavarai maṇantiruntāḷ. avarkaḷ perumpālum cakōtararākavum, tantaiyum putalvarkaḷumāyu miruntaṉar. peṇ pala kaṇavarai maṇakkum vaḻakkam, peṇ tokai kuṟaivatālum āṇ tokai atikappaṭuvatālum uṇṭākiṉṟatu eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar kūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ, iv vaḻakkam iṉṟum vaṭamēṟku amerikka makkaḷ, teṉṉintiya makkaḷ cilar, tipettu, makkaḷiṭaiyē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.
evaṉ peṇṇai mutalil cērkiṉṟāṉō, piṟakkum piḷḷai avaṉuṭaiyateṉap pirittāṉiya makkaḷ koṇṭārkaḷ eṉac cīcar kūṟiyuḷḷār. jermaṉi, ayarlāntu, skāttulāntu mutaliya nāṭukaḷil maṇattilaṉṟaiya iravu peṇṇai naṇparukkuk koṭuppatākiya vaḻakku camīpa kālam varaiyil naṭaipeṟṟatu. itu an nāṭukaḷil palatāra maṇam iruntateṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟākum. peṇ palarai maṇakkum vaḻakkam tāyāṭciyai vaḷarac ceytatu.
jermaṉiyar peṇkaḷaip paricuttamuṭaiyavarkaḷākak karutiṉār kaḷ. avarkaḷ avarkaḷiṉ puttimatiyai allatu avarkaḷ ceyyum tīrppukkaḷait taṭṭamāṭṭārkaḷ eṉṟu tāciṟras eṉṉum paḻaiya carittira āciriyar kūṟiyuḷḷār. mattiya āppirikkā, ciṉṉa āciyā mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷilum tāyāṭci vaḻakku uḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷilum peṇkaḷ iṉṟum talaiyārikaḷākavum kurumārkaḷākavum iruntu varukiṉṟaṉar. tāciṟras kālattil jermaṉiyil āṇkaḷ mukkiya nikaḻccikaḷaip peṇkaḷuṭaṉ kalantu ālōcittārkaḷ. āppirikkāvilē cāmpaci nīkrōvar peṇkaḷ kūṭi ālōcikkum iṭaṅkaḷukkuc cellamāṭṭārkaḷ. avarkaḷ maṉaivi yariṉ cammatamiṉṟip piṟarukku oru utaviyum ceyya māṭṭārkaḷ. paṉs nīkrōvarkaḷukkiṭaiyil āṇkaḷiṉ ālōcaṉaik kūṭṭattil talaiyāriyiṉ tāy kalantu koḷvāḷ.
tantai āṭci
tāyāṭcikku muṉ tantai āṭciyiruntatu. kaṉṟukkuṭṭi māṭṭuk kāraṉukkuc contamāvatupōla piḷḷai tantaikkuc contamāṉavaṉ eṉṉum caṭṭam iruntatu. peṇ, tiruṭappaṭṭu allatu vilai koṭuttu vāṅkappaṭṭuk koṇṭuvarappaṭṭāḷ. celvaṉ allatu palamuṭaiyavaṉ pala peṇkaḷai vaittirukkalām. ivvāṟu ceytal oruvakaik kaṭamai eṉṟu tāciṟras kūṟiyuḷḷār. ayarlāntiṉ uḷnāṭukaḷil peṇkaḷait tiruṭuvatākiya vaḻakkaṅkaḷ pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ naṭuppakutivarai yilum iruntatu. skāntinēviya makkaḷuḷ vaikiṅ (Vikings) āṭavar tamatu atiṭṭattukku ēṟṟavāṟu, iraṇṭu, mūṉṟu allatu pala maṉaiviyarai vaittiruntārkaḷ; celvarum talaiyārikaḷum mikap pala maṉaiviyarai uṭaiyarāyiruntaṉar. oru peṇṇai oruvaṉ vilai koṭuttu vāṅki ṉāl avaṉ avaḷai viṟkavum muṭiyum, iv vaḻakkam jermaṉiyil ciṟu vakuppiṉariṭaiyē patiṉmūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭu varaiyum iruntatu. oruvaṉ virumpiyapōtu taṉatu maṉaiviyaiyum piḷḷaikaḷaiyum viṟṟāṉ.
iṅkilāntilē patiṉeṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṟpakuti varaiyil kaṇavaṉ taṉatu maṉaiviyai avaḷatu cammatamiṉṟiyē tāṉ virumpiya paṭi āṭumāṭukaḷ viṟpatupōla viṟkum urimai peṟṟiruntāṉ. ciṟṟaracaṉ (Duke) oruvaṉ taṉatu vaṇṭi yōṭṭupavaṉiṉ maṉaiviyai vilaikoṭuttu vāṅkiya āvaṇamum, cerupput taippavaṉ kayiṟṟil kaṭṭic cantaikkuk koṇṭu vantu viṟṟa peṇṇai aintu pavaṉukku vāṅkiya āvaṇamum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
kiṟittuva matamum peṇkaḷum
muṟkālattil jermaṉiyil vitavai kollappaṭṭuk kaṇavaṉōṭu putaikkappaṭṭāḷ. kālattil ivvaḻakkaṅkaḷ maṟaintupōka peṇṇuk kuk kaṇavaṉait teriyum urimai uṇṭāyiṟṟu. āṭavaṉiṉ ellaiyil lāta atikāram taḷarntatu. ippoḻutu avaṉ peyaraḷavil peṇṇiṉ pātukāppāḷaṉākaviruntāṉ. patiṉaintām nūṟṟāṇṭil peṇ, cot tukku urimaiyuṭaiyavaḷāṉāḷ. piṉpu kirēkkar urōmarkaḷiṭaiyē nērntavai pōṉṟa māṟṟaṅkaḷ uṇṭāyiṉa. pirāṅkiya (Frankish) makkaḷiṉ oḻukkattaip paṟṟi akkālac caṭṭaṅkaḷē veḷiyiṭukiṉṟaṉa. ak kālattil kurumārōṭu kiṟittuva matattait taḻuviya peṇkaḷ toṭarpu vaittirukka aṉumatikkum camayac caṭṭaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. kiṟittuva matam peṇkaḷiṉ camūka nilaiyai uyarttuvataṟku utavi purintateṉak kūṟa muṭiyātu. toṭakkattil atu araciyal campantamum vāṇika campantamum uṭaiyatāyiruntatu. atu palatāra maṇaṅkaḷaik kaṇṭikkavillai. kiṟittuva āṇṭiṉ muṟpakutiyil pala peṇkaḷai maṇattal pāvamākak koḷḷappaṭavillai. kiṟittuva camayam peṇkaḷukkuc ciṟitum matippuk koṭukkavillai. piṟkālak kurumār, peṇkaḷ acuttamāṉavarkaḷum pāvam niṟaintavarkaḷum eṉak kūṟiṉar.

piṟkālattil kiṟittuva nāṭukaḷil oruvaṉ oru peṇṇai maṇakkum vaḻakkam camūka, poruḷātāra ētukkaḷāl uṇṭāyiṟṟu. pala peṇkaḷai maṇakkum vaḻakkam māṟi oru peṇṇai maṇakkum vaḻakkam uṇṭāṉapōtum vaippāṭṭiyiṉ eṇkaḷaip paṟṟiya kaṭṭuppāṭu irukkavillai. vaippāṭṭikaḷukkup piṟanta piḷḷaikaḷukkuc cottil urimai irukkavillai; avarkaḷ muṟaimaiyāṉa piḷḷaikaḷākavum koḷḷappaṭavillai. itaṉāl mariyātaiyuṭaiya peṟṟōr vaippāṭṭi kaḷiṉ kumārarukkut taṅkaḷ kumārikaḷai maṇam muṭikka virumpavillai. itaṉāl oru peṇṇai maṇakkum vaḻakkam valimaiyaṭaintatu. poruḷātāram ētuvākat tōṉṟiya orutāra maṇattaic camayamum kiriyaikaḷ mūlam vaṟpuṟuttiṟṟu.
muṉpu milittiyā, icis, apiraṭaiṭ mutaliya peyarkaḷāl makkaḷ vaḻaṅkiya kaṉṉit teyvattukkup patil mēri vaḻipāṭu uṇṭāyiṟṟu. itu peṇkaḷiṉ uyarvukku ētuvāyiruntatu.

15ām nūṟṟāṇṭil kiṟittuva nāṭukaḷil oḻukkam
patiṉaintām nūṟṟāṇṭil kiṟittuva nāṭukaḷil oḻukkam mikavum tāḻvaṭaintiruntatu. pirapukkaḷ kātaṟ pāṭalkaḷaip pāṭikkoṇṭu maṇa māṉa peṇkaḷaip peṟa muyaṉṟārkaḷ, maṇamāṉa peṇkaḷum urōmaṉ, kirēkka parattaiyaraip pōlat tamatu kātalai avarkaḷukku viṟṟārkaḷ, ippeṇkaḷum kirēkka parattaiyar pōlat tammai aḻakiya uṭaikaḷāl alaṅkarittum, mēṉi miṉukkup poruḷkaḷaip payaṉpaṭuttiyum, naṟumaṇaṅkaḷaip pūciyum tammai āṭavariṉ kavarccik kēṟṟavarkaḷākkik koṇṭārkaḷ. caṉṉiyācikaḷē (monks) avarkaḷukkup pāṭam kaṟpittārkaḷ.

ak kālattil uyarntavarkaḷiṭaiyum tāḻntavarkaḷiṭaiyum oḻukkam orēvakaiyākaviruntatu. iraṇṭām payas eṉṉum pāppāṇṭa var patiṉaintām nūṟṟāṇṭil vīyaṉṉā nakariṉ oḻukka nilai evvāṟu iruntateṉa eḻutivaittuḷḷār. “makkaḷ ellōrum kātal oḻukkaṅkaḷi lēyē poḻutu pōkkiṉārkaḷ. pakiraṅka viyapicārikaḷiṉ eṇṇikkai atikarittatu. cila peṇkaḷē oru āṭavaṉōṭu tiruptiyaṭaintirun tārkaḷ. pirapukkaḷ, kuṭiyāṉavaṉiṉ aḻakiya maṉaivikkup piṉṉāl ōṭuvatu potu nikaḻcciyāyiruntatu. an niḻaccik kālaṅkaḷil peṇṇiṉ kaṇavaṉē viruntāḷiyākiya pirapuvukkuc cārāyam koṭuttu, avaṉai avaḷōṭu taṉimaiyākak kūṭikkulāvumpaṭi viṭuvāṉ. peṇkaḷ palar tām virumpāta kaṇavarai nañcu koṭuttuk koṉṟaṉar. aracāṅka uttiyōkattar tam maṉaiviyar putalviyarōṭu oḻukuvatai etirtta kuṭiyāṉavarkaḷil palar kollappaṭṭārkaḷ.
viyapicārattukkuk kāraṇam
viyapicāram oruvaṉ oruttiyai maṇappatākiya oru tāra maṇatti ṉāl taṭukkak kūṭiyataṉṟu. atu āṭavaṉiṉ peṇ cērkkai viruppum, peṇṇiṉ āṇ cērkkai viruppum takunta vaḻiyil kaṭṭuppaṭuttap paṭāviṭil uṇṭākiṉṟatu. camūkattil āṭavarilum atikamākap peṇka ḷiruntālum, peṇkaḷilum āṇ mikuntālum itu atikam nikaḻkiṉ ṟatu. iyaṟkaiyāṉa tūṇṭutalkaḷiṉ nimittam āṭavaṉ viyapicārattai virumpum pōtu peṇkaḷ ataṉāl ilāpam aṭaiya muntuvārkaḷ.

mattiya kālattil ivvakaip pala kāraṇaṅkaḷāl viyapicāram atikappaṭṭatu. ataṉviḷaivāka oru tāramaṇam camayattiṉāl kaṭṭāyap paṭuttappaṭṭatu. oruvaṉukkum oruttikkum vāḻnāḷ muḻumaiyilum irukkak kūṭiyatallāta āṇ peṇ uṟavukaḷ pāvamāṉatākavum taṇṭaṉaik kuriyaṉavum eṉṟu karutappaṭṭaṉa.

kiṟittuva matam mutalil kurumār tirumaṇam ceyvatait taṭukka villai; kōvilaic cērnta caṉṉiyācikaḷ, caṉṉiyācip peṇkaḷ māttiram tirumaṇam ceyyat taṭukkappaṭṭiruntārkaḷ. ki.pi.869ām āṇṭu koṇṭuvarappaṭṭa caṭṭattil pala maṉaiviyaraiyuṭaiya kurumār tamatu camaya camapantamāṉa uṭaiyaik kaḷaintuviṭa vēṇṭumeṉac collappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ciṟitu kālattil caṉṉiyācikaḷum caṉṉiyācip peṇkaḷum taṅkum maṭaṅkaḷ kaḷiyāṭṭattukkuriya iṭaṅkaḷākavum, viyapicāra viṭutikaḷākavum māṟiṉa. oḻukkakkēṭukaḷ aḷavu kaṭantu perukiṉa. kurumāriṉ iṉpa nukarvu avarkaḷiṉ nilaiyāṉa cōmpal vāḻkkaiyiṉāl ceḻippaṭaintatu. kurumāriṉ ikaḻccikkuriya oḻukkak kēṭu kāraṇamāka mūṉṟām kirikari (Gregory III), kurumār ellōrum tuṟavikaḷāyiruttal vēṇṭumeṉṉum caṭṭattaik kaṭṭāyamākkiṉār. ippoḻutu āyirakkaṇakkāṉa kurumār tamatu iccaikaḷait tamatu pātukāppilum tamatu atikārattilum iruntavar mūlam niṟaivēṟṟik koṇṭārkaḷ. oḻukkaṅkeṭṭa icceyalkaḷaip paṟṟip pāṭalkaḷum, kataikaḷum, paḻamoḻikaḷum ak kālattu vaḻaṅkiṉa.

mattiya kālattil tirumaṇamiṉṟi alaintu tiriyum oru vakaic caṉṉiyācik kūṭṭattiṉarāl oḻukkak kēṭukaḷ paraviṉa. poṟāmai, piṟar pōṭṭiyiṭuvārkaḷ eṉṟa payam mutaliyavaikaḷāl paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷil aṉṉiyarukku vēlaikaḷ koṭukka maṟukkappaṭṭatu. ak kālaṅkaḷil vāḻkkaiyiṉ poruṭṭum viyapicāram ārampittatu. pōrkaḷil pala āṇkaḷ maṭintaṉar. ciluvaip pōrkaḷil īṭupaṭṭa āṇkaḷil ciṟantavar, pala āṇṭukaḷ ayal nāṭukaḷil taṅkiṉar. āṭavar palar kūṭṭaṅkaḷākac cērntu ayalnāṭukaḷiṟ ceṉṟu vāṇikam naṭattiṉar. ellāp paṭṭiṉaṅ kaḷilum vāḻum makkaḷil perumpālōr paruvamaṭainta peṇkaḷā yiruntaṉar. itu kāraṇamāka ‘peṇkaḷiṉ viṭutikaḷ’ eṉṉum peyaruṭaṉ viyapicāra nilaiyaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. iv viṭutikaḷukku varikaḷ, eḻuttu mūlamuḷḷa kaṇakkukaḷaik koṇṭu vitikkappaṭṭaṉa. iv viṭutikaḷil vāḻum peṇkaḷiṉ cārpākat taṉic caṭṭaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. iv viṭutiyaic cērātu taṉippaṭṭa viyapicārattāl tammōṭu pōṭṭiyiṭum peṇkaḷ mītu ivarkaḷ vaḻakkut toṭukkak kūṭiyatākaviruntatu. ki.pi.1,414il kāṉsṭaṉs (Constance) eṉṉum iṭattil kūṭiya makānāṭṭiṉpōtu kiṭaikkakkūṭiya ūtiyattai etirpārttu avviṭattil 1,500 ivvakaip parattaip peṇkaḷ tiraṇṭārkaḷ, cāḷs (Charles) eṉpavaṉiṉ paṭṭāḷattil 900 kurumārum, 1,600 parattaip peṇkaḷum iruntārkaḷ. urōmaṉ irāṇikaḷ matuk kaṭaikaḷukkuc ceṉṟu tammai muṭṭākkiṭṭu maṟaittuk koṇṭu parattaimaiyāl tamatu iccaikaḷait taṇittuk koṇṭārkaḷ.



parattaimai ellāk kālaṅkaḷilum pāvamuṭaiyatākak koḷḷap paṭṭatu. cila camayaṅkaḷil “kiṟittuva matattukku nērum palavakait tīmai kaḷaip pōkkuvatu kāraṇamāka ik kuṟṟam paricutta tēvālayattāl maṉṉikkappaṭukiṉṟatu” ivvāṟu nēṇpek eṉṉumiṭattil patiṉain tām nūṟṟāṇṭil kūṭiya nakara makānāṭṭil kūṟappaṭṭatu. kiṟittuva matattil periya puraṭciyait tōṟṟuvitta ulūtar, āṇ cērkkai campantamākat tammait tiruptip paṭuttik koḷvataṟkup peṇkaḷukkup pūraṇa urimaiyuṇṭu eṉṟum, uṇpatu, uṟaṅkuvatu, viṭāykku nīr kuṭippatu pōla atu avaḷukku avaciyamāṉavaikaḷuḷ oṉṟu eṉṟum, ivai kaḷukku māṟākat talaiyiṭukiṉṟavaṉ iyaṟkaikku icaiya naṭakkātava ṉeṉṟum, neruppuc cuṭāteṉṟum, taṇṇīr naṉaikkāteṉṟum, maṉitaṉ uṇṇak kuṭikka nittirai koḷḷa māṭṭāṉeṉṟum niṉaippatu tavaṟu eṉṟum kūṟiyuḷḷār.

paḻaiya epirēyaruḷ oruvaṉ putalvaṉillāmal iṟantu viṭṭāl avaṉuṭaiya vitavaiyai avaṉuṭaiya cakōtaraṉ, putalvaṉ piṟakkum varaiyil cērntirukkalām eṉṉum camayaviti uṇṭu. ivvaḻakkam niyōkam eṉṉum peyaruṭaṉ pañcāppil naṭaipeṟukiṉṟatu. maṅkōli yar, caipīriyar mutaliya makkaḷiṭaiyēyum ivvaḻakkam iruntu varukiṉ ṟatu. iv vaḻakkattait taḻuvip pōlum ulūtar, āṇmai (Impotent) aṟṟavaṉai maṇantavarkaḷil itu maṉṉikkattakkatākaviruntatu. orutti kaṇavaṉiṉ cakōtaraṉ allatu avaṉuṭaiya kiṭṭiya uṟaviṉaṉaic cēralām eṉac camayac caṭṭam koṇṭu vantār. āṇmaiyillāta oruvaṉ taṉatu maṉaiviyuṭaṉ ayal vīṭṭukkāraṉiṭañceṉṟu taṉatu maṉaiviyiṉ kātal viruppattai niṟaivēṟṟa vēṇṭumeṉak kēṭka vēṇṭum. avaṉ ataṟku uṭaṉpaṭāviṭṭāl avaṉ taṉatu maṉaiviyai naṉṟāka uṭutti kiṭṭiya cantaikkuc cella vēṇṭum. aṅku avaḷ taṉatu kaṇavaṉukkup piḷḷaiyaip peṟṟu aḷippataṟku utaviyāyuḷḷa oru kātalaṉaik kaṇṭupiṭikkak kūṭum eṉap paḻaiya jermaṉ āciriyar oruvar ak kālattil jermaṉiyil irunta vaḻakkattaik kuṟippiṭ ṭuḷḷār. ulūtar muṉṉirunta ivvitiyaip piṉpaṟṟit taṉatu vitiyaik kūṟiyirukkalām. peṇṇukkum āṇukkum cama urimaiyiṉ poruṭṭu nērnta pōril ulūtariṉ karuttup peṇkaḷukkuc cārpākavuḷḷatu.

patiṉēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ naṭuppakuti peṇkaḷ viḻippaṭainta kālamākum. appoḻutu alaṅkāram (fashion) eṉṉum eḻucci peṇka ḷiṭaiyē eḻuntatu. mika alaṅkāriyākiya peṇ taṉṉai alaṅkārañ ceytu koḷvataṟku nāḷil ēḻumaṇi nēram celaviṭṭāḷ. peṇkaḷ iyaṟkaiyākak kavarccikkuriya aḻaku vāyntavarkaḷāyirāviṭṭālum āṭavaṉaik kavaruvataṟku allatu camūkattil matippaṭaivataṟkuk kalvi kaṟkiṉṟārkaḷ. muṉ kūṟappaṭṭa kirēkka parattaiyar kūṭṭattiṉarālum im muṟai kaiyāḷappaṭṭatu. patiṉāṟām nūṟṟāṇṭil naṭuppakutiyiliruntu ittāliyil kalvi peṇkaḷiṉ cītaṉamākak karutappaṭṭatu.

peṇkaḷiṉ viṭutalai ciṟitu ciṟitāka vaḷarntu koṇṭu vantatu. ataṉai evvakaiyilum āṭavarāl taṭukka muṭiyavillai. iṭaiyiṭaiyē peṇkaḷiṉ viṭutalai campantamāṉa kiḷarccikaḷ, varalāṟṟil iṭam peṟṟa palarāl naṭattappaṭṭu vantaṉa. poruḷātāra araciyal kāraṇaṅkaḷāl peṇkaḷiṉ viṭutalai pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭil ēṟpaṭṭatu. ataṉāl peṇkaḷ vāṇikattuṟaiyil āṇkaḷai otta urimai aṭaintārkaḷ. toṭakkattil ellā makkaḷiṭaiyum peṇkaḷ camūkattukkuriya virumpat takāta ellāk kaṭiṉa vēlaikaḷaiyum puriyumpaṭi kaṭṭāyappaṭuttappaṭ ṭārkaḷ. avarkaḷ nūl nūṟṟal, necavu neytal, taittal, cavukkārañ ceytal pōṉṟa toḻilkaḷaiyum ceytārkaḷ. poruḷātāra nerukkaṭi yiṉāl avarkaḷ ippoḻutu peṇkaḷukku uriyavaṉavallāta vēlai kaḷaiyum puriyalāṉārkaḷ. ivai nilaimaiyai māṟutal aṭaiyacceytaṉa. ippoḻutu peṇkaḷ, āṇkaḷukku māttiram viṭappaṭṭirunta kait toḻil, vāṇikam mutaliyavaikaḷaiyum ceytu ūtiyam aṭaintārkaḷ. iṅkilāntilē patiṉeṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyil vāḻkkaip pōrāṭṭattiṉ nerukkaṭiyiṉāl peṇkaḷiṉ viṭutalaip pōr aṉukūlamāka naṭaipeṟṟatu. nūl nūṟkavum, necavu ceyyavum, āṭaikku accaṭikkavum iyantirat toḻiṟcālaikaḷil oru ilaṭcam peṇkaḷum eṇpatiṉāyiram ciṟuvarkaḷum vēlai ceytārkaḷ, ippeṇkaḷum ciṟuvarkaḷum mikavum mōcamāṉa vāḻkkai naṭatti vantārkaḷ.

1907il jermaṉiyil māttiramē 2,10,000 peṇkaḷ toḻiṟcālai kaḷil vēlai ceytārkaḷ. ivarkaḷil nūṟṟukkup patiṉmūṉṟu vītattiṉar maṇamāṉavarkaḷ. āṇkaḷilum pārkkap peṇkaḷiṉ vēlai tiṟamai yaṟṟatākaviruntamaiyāl peṇkaḷiṉ kūli kuṟaivāyiruntatu. ōrōriṭaṅkaḷil itaṟku māṟākavum iruntu vantatu. amerikkāvil itu ivvāṟu iruntatu. amerikkāvilē meyiṉ (Maine) pōṉṟa toḻiṟ cālaikaḷ mikunta paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷ “peṇkaḷiṉ paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷ” eṉṟu aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. aṅku āṇkaḷ camaiyal, tuṇitōyttal, kuḻantai kaḷaik kavaṉittal pōṉṟa vīṭṭu vēlaikaḷaip pārppārkaḷ. maṉaivi mār toḻiṟcālaikaḷil āṭavarkaḷaip pārkkilum atika kūli peṟ ṟārkaḷ. ātalāl āṭavar peṇkaḷ ceyyum vīṭṭu vēlaikaḷaic ceytu vantārkaḷ. amerikkāvilē muṟpōkkil uyarntu ceṉṟa peṇkaḷ oru taṉippaṭṭa peṇ āṭciyai naṭattukiṟārkaḷ. itu peṇkaḷ kuṟaivāyirunta muṟkālattil peṇkaḷ āṭavarait tamatu eṇṇattiṉ paṭi naṭattiya vaḻakkattiṉ uyirppēyākum. airōppiya makkaḷukku in nilaimai iyaṟkaikku māṟupaṭṭatupōl kāṇappaṭalām, iṉṟum amerikkāvil āṇkaḷaiviṭap peṇkaḷ kuṟaivāyiruppatāl in nilaimai aṅku kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. aṅku nāṉku kōṭi peṇkaḷum nāṉku kōṭiyē nāṟpatu ilaṭcam āṇkaḷum uḷḷārkaḷ. peṇkaḷ poruḷātāra viṭutalaiyiṉāl iṉṟu ellāt tuṟaikaḷilum āṇkaḷai otta cama urimai aṭaintuḷḷārkaḷ. ellā nākarika cātiyārum peṇ kaḷum āṇkaḷum orē urimai uḷḷavarkaḷ eṉpatai ēṟṟuk koḷ kiṉṟaṉar. iṉṟu viṭutalai aṭainta peṇkaḷiṉ oḻukka nilai, iraṇ ṭāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ kēṭō eṉṉum urōmāṉiyar kūṟiyatai oppa āṭavarukkup payam viḷaippatāyirukkiṉṟatu. iṉṟu nām atē vaḻikkuttāṉ ceṉṟu koṇṭirukkiṟōm. nām kēṭō kūṟiyatai otta nilaiyai aṭaivataṟkuc ciṟitukālam cellalām eṉa oru cīrtirutta vāti kūṟiyuḷḷār.

tirumaṇam (mēṟkut tēcaṅkaḷil)
tirumaṇam kuṭumpa nikaḻcciyilum pārkkac camūka nikaḻcciyākak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. tirumaṇattiṉāl maṟṟoru putiya kuṭumpam uṇṭā kiṉṟatu. āṭavaṉ oruvaṉatum, peṇ oruvaḷatum vāḻnāḷil tirumaṇam mikac ciṟappuṭaiyatātaliṉ atu mukkiyattuvamuṭaiyatākak koḷḷappaṭu kiṉṟatu. muṟkālattil intiya āṭavar tāmē tamatu maṉaiviyarait terintukoṇṭaṉar. piṟkālattil icceyal peṟṟōriṉ kaṭamaiyāka māṟiviṭṭatu. airōppiya nāṭukaḷil peṟṟōr tamatu putalvaraiyum putalviyaiyum tāmākavē tamatu maṉaiviyaraiyum kaṇavaraiyum terintu koḷḷa aṉumatikkiṉṟaṉar. āṇ peṇṇaiyō, peṇ āṇaiyō terivataṉ muṉ iruvarum cilakālam paḻakukiṟārkaḷ. itu āṭavaṉāl tāṉ ārampikkappaṭukiṉṟatu. vikṭōriyā irāṇi kālattil, peṇṇiṉ tantai taṉatu cammatattait terivittālaṉṟi āṭavaṉ avaḷōṭu paḻakutal muṭiyātu. peṇ “ām” eṉṟu taṉatu tirumaṇa viruppattait terivittatum terivu muṭivaṭaikiṉṟatu. itu ‘vārttaip pāṭu’ (Proposal) eṉṉum mariyātaiyāṉa peyaraip peṟukiṉṟatu. peṇṇiṉ tantai paḻaku vataṟku aṉumatittal, peṇ avaṉai ēṟṟukkoḷḷutal vēṇṭum eṉṉum kaṭṭuppāṭu ākamāṭṭātu. iṉṟu iḷaiñaṉ oruvaṉ taṉakku viruppamāṉa iḷam peṇ oruttiyōṭu pēcip paḻakuvataṟku avaḷ tantaiyiṭamiruntu aṉumati peṟavēṇṭiyatillai. cila kālattiṟku muṉ oruvaṉ peṇṇōṭu paḻaka vēṇṭumāṉāl takka oruvar avaṉai avaḷukku aṟimukappaṭutta vēṇṭum; allāta iḷaiñaṉōṭu peṇ pēcamāṭṭāḷ. iṉṟu ivvāṟu evarum aṟimukappaṭutta vēṇṭiya tēvai illai. peṇṇum āṇum virumpiṉāl pēcik koḷḷalām. avarkaḷait taṭaippaṭuttuvār yārum illai. iṉṟu peṇkaḷ vayatu mutirnta peṇkaḷiṉ tuṇai iṉṟi veḷiyē celkiṟārkaḷ. muṟkālat til ivvāṟu nikaḻavillai. ivarkaḷai ellā iṭaṅkaḷilum taṉimaiyil cantikkalām. naṭaṉamāṭum maṇṭapattil allatu ciṟṟuṇṭic cālaiyil irukkum peṇṇiṭam ceṉṟu avaḷuṭaṉ irukkum peṇ allatu āṇukkuk kuṉintu vaṇakkañ ceytapiṉ avaḷai naṭittuk kaḷiyāṭṭam āṭuvataṟku aḻaippatu vaḻakkattukku māṟāṉa ceyalākak karutappaṭa māṭṭātu. iṉṟum āṇaip peṇṇukku aṟimukappaṭutta vēṇṭum eṉṉum koḷkaiyuṭaiyavarkaḷ irukkiṟārkaḷ; āṉāl avarkaḷ mikac cilarē. āṭavaṉ aḻakum mariyātaiyum uṭaiyavaṉākak kāṇappaṭṭāl peṇ aṟimukappaṭuttuvatait tēvaippaṭamāṭṭāḷ. paḻaiya nāḷil paḻakutal eṉpatu āṇaip poṟuttiruntatu. peṇ orupōtum taṉatu eṇṇattai mutalil aṟivippatillai.

vikṭōriyā kālattil peṇkaḷ peritum veḷiyē cella villai. ākavē avarkaḷ āṭavarōṭu atikam paḻakavillai. ippoḻutu peṇkaḷukkut tām aṟiya vēṇṭiya ellāraip paṟṟiyum, ellāk karumaṅkaḷaip paṟṟiyum teriyum. ākavē avarkaḷukku āṭavar vakuppiṉaraip paṟṟiya mayakkam yātum illai. avarkaḷ vālipariṉ tēṉ pōṉṟu iṉippāṉa pēccukkaḷāl eṭupaṭamāṭṭārkaḷ; pakaṭṭākat tōṉṟukiṉṟavaikaḷil avarkaḷ nampikkai koḷvatum illai. ākavē avarkaḷai makiḻvippatu kaṭiṉam. iṉṟaiya vālipaṉukkup peṇṇōṭu paḻakuvatu villaṅkamākat tōṉṟum. paḻaiya pōkkil avaḷiṉ tirumaṇa viruppattaik kēṭṭāl avaḷ uṭaṉē kekkaṭṭam iṭṭuc cirippāḷ. ikkālap peṇṇiṭattil, pēcum aṟivu atikam uṇṭu; avaḷ akaṉṟa aṟivum, kaṭamaikaḷai niṟaivēṟṟuvatil uṟuti yum uṭaiyavaḷ. āviyāka māṟiviṭakkūṭiya kātaliṉālum pakaṭṭiṉālum makiḻcciyōṭu vāḻamuṭiyāteṉṟu avaḷukkut teriyum. kuṟikkappaṭṭa oruvaṉai maṇappatāl makiḻcciyum, vāḻkkaiyil vāyppukkaḷum irukkumeṉak kaṇṭāl avaḷ tirumaṇattukku icaikiṉṟāḷ. iṉṟu peṇ orutti kūṟuvatu eṉṉaveṉṟāl, “uṉṉaik kātalikkum oruvaṉai aṉṟu, nī kātalikkum oruvaṉai eṉpatākum.

ikkālattil āṭavaṉ oruvaṉ taṉatu kātalai aṟivikkum poruṭṭu marattiṉ kīḻ oruttiyiṉ kaiyaip piṭippatu allatu pulliṉ mītu muḻaṅkāl paṭivatu ippoḻutu illai; eṉṉai maṇañ ceykiṉṟāyā eṉṟu veḷippaṭaiyākavum nāṇamiṉṟiyum kēṭkalām. “ceyvēṉ” eṉṟu nāṇamiṉṟiyum maṉa acaiviṉṟiyum peṇ kūṟalām. tirumaṇat tukku oruttiyai icaivupaṭuttuvataṟku avaḷaic cuṟṟit tirivatākiya paḻaiya pōkku viraivil māṟupaṭṭu varukiṉṟatu. kātal, pakaṭṭu eṉpaṉa tamatu kavarcciyai iḻantuviṭṭaṉa eṉṟāvatu kuṭumpa cēmattukkākavum kātal nukarccikkākavum tirumaṇaṅkaḷ ceyyappaṭavillai eṉṟāvatu karututal ākātu. kātaliṉāl pala tirumaṇaṅkaḷ nikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. mēṟkut tēcat tirumaṇaṅkaḷait tōlvi eṉṟu karutiviṭutal ākātu. makiḻcci yaḷiyāta tirumaṇattukku maruntu tirumaṇa nīkkam.
oru āṭavaṉum peṇṇum maṇam muṭikka icaintatum peṇ taṉatu iṭatukai mōtira viralil kalpatitta mōtiramoṉṟai aṇintu koḷkiṉṟāḷ. immōtiram avaḷai maṇañ ceyyappōkum āṭava ṉāl aḷikkappaṭṭatu. peṟṟōriṉ viruppam kēṭkappaṭukiṉṟatu. matip pukkuriya kuṭumpaṅkaḷil orunāḷ kuṟikkappaṭukiṉṟatu. aṉṟu uṟaviṉariṉ muṉṉāl tirumaṇam niccayamāṉa ceyti kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. aṉṟu viruntum naṭaṉamum uṇṭu.

tirumaṇattukku nāḷ kuṟikkappaṭukiṉṟatu. mēl nāṭṭavar nāḷ pārppatillai. avarkaḷukku oru nāḷaip pōlavē ellā nāṭkaḷum nallavai. tirumaṇam kālai eṭṭu allatu oṉpatu maṇikkum mālai mūṉṟu maṇikkum iṭaiyil nikaḻum. mūṉṟu maṇikkup piṉ tirumaṇam nikaḻvatillai. mēl nāṭukaḷil tirumaṇap pativu nilaiyaṅkaḷil tirumaṇam ceytu koḷḷalām. maṇamakaṉum maṇamakaḷum tirumaṇap pativu nilaiyattukku iraṇṭu allatu mūṉṟu cāṭcikaḷuṭaṉ celvārkaḷ. pativu ceypavar avarkaḷ kaṇavaṉ maṉaiviyarāka vāḻa virumpukiṉṟaṉarā eṉakkēṭpār. ‘ām’ eṉṟu maṟumoḻi varumāyiṉ tirumaṇam pativu ceytu koḷḷappaṭum. maṇamakaṉ, maṇamakaḷ eṉpōr avarkaḷtāṉ eṉṟu attāṭcip paṭuttuvataṟkum tirumaṇattukkuc cāṭci aḷippataṟku māka uṭaṉ ceṉṟavarkaḷ utaviyaḷippar. pativukārar putiya kaṇavaṉ maṉaiviyarukku tirumaṇa attāṭcic cīṭṭuk koṭuppār. muṉ oru muṟai kaliyāṇa nīkkam ceytavarkaḷum ēḻaikaḷum perumpālum ivvāṟu tirumaṇattai muṭittukkoḷkiṉṟaṉar.

mēl nāṭṭil tirumaṇak koṇṭāṭṭam curukkamāṉatu. atu cila maṇi nēramē naṭaipeṟukiṉṟatu. mikakkiṭṭiya naṇparum cuṟṟattavarkaḷu mallāta piṟar maṇamakaḷ allatu maṇamakaḷ vīṭṭil taṅkiyirukkac celva tillai. mutalnāḷ mālai allatu tirumaṇattaṉṟu kālaiyil maṇa makaṉ tirumaṇam naṭakkum iṭattukku varuvāṉ; āṉāl avaṉ maṇamaka ḷiṉ vīṭṭil taṅkamāṭṭāṉ. maṇamakaṉ taṉatu cuṟṟattavar kaḷōṭum naṇparkaḷōṭum kōyilukkuc celvāṉ. avaṉ aṅku maṇamakaḷiṉ varavai etirpārttiruppāṉ. maṇamakaḷ taṉatu tantai cakōtarar mutaliya varkaḷōṭu avviṭattukku varuvāḷ. maṇamakaṉ avaḷaik kōyil vāyilil etirkoṇṭu aḻaittuk koṇṭu palipīṭattukkuc celvāṉ. maṇamakaḷiṉ pakkattār iṭappakkattilum maṇamakaḷiṉ pakkattār valappakkattilum iruppārkaḷ. maṇamakaḷ maṇamakaṉukku iṭappakkattil muḻaṅkāl paṭintu niṟpāḷ. iruvarum varumpōtu kōyiluḷḷa ellārum eḻuntu niṟpārkaḷ. tirumaṇak kiriyai nīṇṭataṉṟu, avarkaḷ kaṇavaṉ maṉaiviyarāka virumpukiṟārkaḷō eṉṟu kuru kēṭkiṟār. ellōrum avarkaḷ kūṟum maṟumoḻiyai āvalōṭu etirpārkkiṟārkaḷ. iru
varum “nāṉ virumpukiṟēṉ” eṉṟu taṉittaṉiyē kūṟuvārkaḷ. uṭaṉē maṇamakaṉ tirumaṇa mōtirattaip peṇṇiṉ iṭatu viralil aṇikiṉṟāṉ. intiya makkaḷ tāli tarippatu pōṉṟatu avarkaḷ mōtiram tarippatu. piṉpu kuruvāl tirumaṇap pativuc cīṭṭu ācīrvātattuṭaṉ avarkaḷ kaiyil aḷikkappaṭukiṟatu. maṇamakaṉum maṇamakaḷum kōyilukku veḷiyē varutalum avarkaḷ makiḻcci āravārattuṭaṉ vara vēṟkappaṭuvārkaḷ. piṉpu kaliyāṇak kūṭṭam maṇamakaḷiṉ vīṭṭukkuc cellum. kaliyāṇa viruntu mika makiḻvukkuriyatu. mika nērttiyāṉavum urukiyuḷḷavumāṉa uṇavup poruḷkaḷ matu vakaiyum parimāṟappaṭukiṉ ṟaṉa. “tirumaṇa appam” (Wedding Cake) avaikaḷuḷ mukkiyamuṭaiyatu. maṇamakaḷ appattai veṭṭuvāḷ; maṇamakaṉ avaḷiṭam kattiyai eṭuttuk koṭuppāṉ. appam viruntiṉarukkup parimāṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. viruntiṉar maṇamakaṉ maṇamakaḷ eṉṉum iruvariṉ uṭal nalaṉ naṉṟā yirukka vēṇṭumeṉa āci kūṟi ataṉai uṇkiṉṟaṉar. viruntukku varāta naṇparkaḷukkum cuṟṟattavarkaḷukkum appattuṇṭukaḷ aṉuppap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. viruntiṉar matu vakaikaḷaik kuṭikkum pōtum maṇamakaḷa tum maṇamakaṉatum uṭalnalaṉ naṉṟāyirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu col vārkaḷ. kuṭivakaikaḷ aruntumpōtu ciṟiya coṟpoḻivukaḷum naṭattap paṭum. viruntu muṭintatum putiya kaṇavaṉum maṉaiviyum “haṉimūṉu”kkup puṟappaṭuvar. ellōrum avarkaḷaip pārttu avarkaḷukku ulakil ellā makiḻcciyum nalaṉumuṇṭāka vēṇṭumeṉṟu āci kūṟuvar.

“haṉimūṉ” mōṭṭār puṟappaṭṭuc celkiṉṟatu. makiḻccic cirippum āravāramum ākāyattil ōcaiyum eḻukiṉṟaṉa. mōṭṭāriṉ piṉpuṟattil paḻaiya cappāttum atiṭṭam tarum kutirai ilāṭamum toṅkukiṉṟaṉa. ivai mikap paḻaṅkālac caṅkatikaḷ.

putiya kaṇavaṉ maṉaiviyar iyalpuṭaiyavarkaḷāyiṉ haṉimūṉai ayalnāṭukaḷil koṇṭāṭuvar. vaṟiyavar tamatu haṉimūṉait tamatu ūrilē allatu tamatu vīṭṭilē koṇṭāṭuvar. haṉimūṉ mika makiḻc cikkum iṉpattukkum uriyateṉak koḷḷappaṭukiṉṟatu. uṇmaiyil oruvarai oruvar kātalikkum maṇamakkaḷukku atu ivvāṟē uḷḷatu. cila camaṅkaḷil haṉimūṉ “kaṉiyāta mūṉāka” (Blue Moon) māṟi viṭuvatu muṇṭu. tirumaṇac celavu perumpālum maṇamakaṉaip poṟuttatu. maṇamakaṉukku koṭukkum kaliyāṇa vekumati, maṇamakaḷiṉ uṭai campantamāṉa ciṟiya celavukaḷē peṇ vīṭṭāraip poṟuttatu.

iṉṟu peṇkaḷ vēṇṭuvatu
1939il kūṭiya peṇkaḷ capai1 oṉṟil intiyap peṇkaḷiṉ camūkanilai evvāṟu iruttal vēṇṭumeṉpataik kuṟittu niṟai vēṟṟappaṭṭa tīrmāṉaṅkaḷiṉ curukkattai iṅkut tarukiṉṟōm.
1. peṇkaḷiṉ nilai ellāvakaiyilum āṇkaḷukkuc camamā yiruttal vēṇṭum.

2. peṇkaḷ eṉṉum kāraṇamāka avarkaḷukku evvakai vēlai kaḷum maṟukkappaṭutal kūṭātu.

3. peṇkaḷ āṇkaḷōṭu camamāṉa camūkap poruḷātāra urimai kaḷaip peṟṟu vāḻvataṟkut tirumaṇam taṭaiyāyiruttal ākātu.

4. aracāṅkam peṇṇaiyum āṇaiyum cama urimaiyuḷḷa aṅkattavarkaḷākak koḷḷutal vēṇṭum.

peṇkaḷukku vākkurimai vēṇṭum. āṭavaraippōla avar kaḷum uttiyōkaṅkaḷ peṟṟu, āṭavaraippōl ūtiyam aṭaital vēṇṭum. vayatāṉa peṇkaḷum āṇkaḷum virumpiṉāl maṇantu koḷvataṟkuc cāti matam eṉṉum kaṭṭuppāṭukaḷ iruttal kūṭātu. peṇkaḷ paruvamaṭaivataṉ muṉ maṇañ ceytukoḷvataic caṭṭam taṭaiceytal vēṇṭum. orē kālattil oruvaṉ oruttiyaiyum, orutti oruvaṉaiyum māttiram maṇam ceytukoḷḷa vēṇṭum. kaliyāṇa nīkka urimai iruttal vēṇṭum. tirumaṇaṅkaḷ pativu ceytukoḷḷum nilai yaṅkaḷ kirāmaṅkaḷtōṟum iruttal vēṇṭum. kaliyāṇa nīkkam iruvaril evar virumpiṉālum ceytu koḷḷalām. āṉāl piḷḷaikaḷiṉ parāmarippu, pātukāppu eṉpavaikaḷaippaṟṟiya ēṟpāṭukaḷ oḻuṅku ceyyap paṭutal vēṇṭum. vitavaiyāyiruppatu kaliyāṇattukku taṭaiyātal kūṭātu. āṭavaraippōlap peṇkaḷukkuc cottu urimaiyum avaṟṟai avarkaḷ vēṇṭiyapaṭi ceyyum atikāramum vēṇṭum. ciṟuvarum ciṟumiyarum kalantu kalvi payilum kalavaṉ pāṭacālaikaḷ vēṇṭum.

ikkālat tāy
muṟkālattil tāyiṉ kaṭamai piḷḷaikaḷaip peṟuvatu māttiram eṉṟu koḷḷappaṭṭatu. avaḷ peṟum piḷḷaikaḷ peṇkaḷākavum, maṉitarākavum evvakaiyilō vaḷarntu viṭuvārkaḷ eṉṉum eṇṇam avarkaḷiṭam iruntatu. taṅkaḷ kuḻantaikaḷai evvāṟu uruppaṭutta vēṇṭumeṉpatu avarkaḷ mūḷaiyiṟpaṭavillai. ikkālat tāy paḻaṅ kālat tāyilum pārkkac camūkap paḻakkamuṭaiyavaḷ. ippoḻutu avaḷ tāṉ ulaka karumaṅkaḷil kalantukoḷḷātu taṉiyēyirukkalāmeṉa uṇaravillai. avaḷ taṉṉaic cuṟṟiyuḷḷa ulaka naṭavaṭikkaikaḷil kalantu koḷkiṟāḷ. piṟantatu mutal kuḻantai evvāṟu payiṟṟappaṭukiṉ ṟatō avvāṟākavē ataṉ piṟkāla vāḻkkaiyum uruppaṭukiṉṟatu. tāy māraiyum kuḻantai vaḷarppaip paṟṟiyum eḻutappaṭṭa pala puttakaṅkaḷai ikkālat tāy paṭikkiṟāḷ. tāymārkaḷaiyum piḷḷaikaḷaiyum paṟṟiya pala ceytikaḷ tiṉamum pēcappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iv viṣayaṅkaḷ muṟkālat tāymārukku iṭakkarākat tōṉṟakkūṭiyaṉa. ivvakaiyāṉa pēccukkaḷ tūymaiyaṟṟaṉaveṉṟu iṉṟu karutappaṭuvatillai. iṉṟaiya nilaimai tāyiṉ kaṭamai piḷḷaikaḷaip peṟuvatu eṉṟa aḷavil irātu avaḷ kaṭamai maṉita camūkattaiyē nalvaḻiyil paḻakki, varuṅkāla ulakattukku nampikkai aḷippatu eṉṟu karutappaṭukiṉṟatu.



peṇkaḷ ulakam
muṉṉurai
ivvulakil nērpakutiyiṉar peṇkaḷ. ulaka varalāṟṟil ivarkaḷ pāti iṭam peṟutal vēṇṭum. āṉāl ulaka varalāṟu eṉpatu āṭavariṉ varalā ṟākavē amaintuḷḷatu. iṭaiyiṭaiyē ovvoru kālattu viḷaṅkiya nāṭṭiyakkāriyō, araciyō, oru paṭaiyait tāṅkic ceṉṟa oruttiyō pōṉṟa peṇkaḷ cilariṉ varalāṟukaḷē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. peṇkaḷē ivvulakil nākarikattait tōṟṟuvit tārkaḷ eṉpatai ārāycciyāḷar orutalaiyāka oppukkoḷkiṉṟaṉar1. “toṭṭilāṭṭuṅkaiyē aracāḷukiṉṟatu”2, “nākarikameṉpatu nalla peṇ kaḷiṉ ūkkutalē”,3 eṉa aṟivāḷikaḷ pukaṉṟiruk kiṉṟaṉar. ivvuṇmaikaḷai aṟiyātum, āṭavarai oppap peṇkaḷum cutantiramuṭaiyavarkaḷ eṉpatai maṟantum, peṇ camūttiṉarukku āṇ camūkattiṉar iḻaittu varum aṭakku muṟaikaḷ nītiyaṟṟaṉa eṉpatai viḷakki in nūl eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷatu. varalāṟṟu muṟaiyākap peṇkaḷiṉ uyarvai viḷakkum ceytikaḷ palavaṟṟai in nūliṟ kāṇalām.
na.ci.kantaiyā






peṇkaḷ perumai
tōṟṟuvāy
maṉita vakuppukkuḷ māttiramaṉṟu, maram, ceṭi, paṟavai, vilaṅkukaḷ mutaliya aḵṟiṇaip poruḷkaḷuḷḷum peṇ uyirkaḷē mikappaya ṉuṭaiyaṉa. ōr āṇ paṉaiyaiyum, ōr peṇpaṉaiyaiyum eṭuttuk koḷvōm. ivaṟṟuḷ peṇ paṉaiyē mikka payaṉuṭaiyateṉpatu evarukkum teḷḷitiṟ pulappaṭum. vilaṅku, paṟavaikaḷuḷ āṭu, kōḻi eṉpavaikaḷiṉ āṇ peṇ iṉaṅkaḷaiyum eṭuttu nōkki avai kaḷuḷ evai payaṉuṭaiyaṉa eṉa aṟintu koḷḷuṅkaḷ. uyirkaḷil peṇṇiṉaṅkaḷē ellā vakaiyilum ciṟantu viḷaṅkum iyaṟkaip paṇpuṭaiyaṉavāyiṉum, makkaḷ camūkattil ivviti māṟupaṭṭatu pōla namakkut tōṉṟukiṟatu. makkaḷuḷ peṇkaḷē āṇkaḷaiviṭa uyarnta taṉmaiyuṭaiyavarkaḷāyiṉ avarkaḷ iṉṟu āṇkaḷukku aṭaṅki naṭakkavēṇṭiya nilaimai evvāṟu uṇṭāyiṟṟu? avarkaḷ iyaṟkai vitikku amaiya āṇkaḷaiviṭac ciṟantu viḷaṅkiṉārkaḷā? avarkaḷ āṇkaḷukku aṭaṅki naṭakkum kaṭṭāyam evvāṟu nērntatu? eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa karuttukkaḷai ārāyntu aṟiyiṉ nām peṇ camūkattiṉariṉ varalāṟṟaiyē aṟintavarkaḷ āvōm. peṇ camūkattiṉariṉ uṇmai varalāṟṟiṉai aṟiyātiruppataṉālēyē, nām peṇkaḷait tāḻnta varkaḷākak karutukiṉṟōm. uṇmaiyil peṇkaḷē āṭavaraiviṭa ellā vakaiyilum matikkattakkavarkaḷāvar. avarkaḷiṉ perumaiyai uṇarntu nām avarkaḷukku mutaṉmai aḷiyāta aḷavil namatu camūkam muṉṉēṟa vakai iṉṟākum. ivaiyum ivaipōṉṟa karuttukkaḷaiyum varalāṟṟōṭum iyaṟkaiyōṭum toṭarpuppaṭutti viḷakkuvatu innūliṉ nōkkamākum.
paṭaippukkaḷ
ivvulakil uyartiṇai, aḵṟiṇai eṉṉum uyirp paṭaippukkaḷ ellām āṇ peṇ eṉṉum iruvakaippaṭum. āṇ peṇ cērkkai yiṉāl ovvoru āṇum peṇṇum tampōṉṟa uyirkaḷait tōṟṟuvittup perukkukiṉṟaṉa. tampōṉṟa uyirkaḷait tōṟṟuvikkum vaṉmaiyai iḻakkum pōtu uyirkaḷ maṭintu pōkiṉṟaṉa. ellā uyirkaḷiṉ vāḻkkai muṟaikaḷaiyum nām ūṉṟi ārāyumiṭattu, uyirp paṭaippukkaḷiṉ nōkkam, tam pōṉṟa uyirkaḷait tōṟṟuvittu ulakil parappuvatē eṉṟu namakku naṉku tōṉṟum. itaṟkākavē avai vāḻkiṉṟaṉa.
vāḻkkaip pōr
marañ ceṭi puḻukkaḷ mutal maṉitaṉ varaiyiluḷḷa ellā uyir kaḷum vāḻkkaiyiṉ poruṭṭu ivvulakil iṭaiviṭātu, pōrāṭik koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. vāḻkkaip pōrāṭṭameṉpatu tām nalluṇavu peṟutalōṭu tammaip piṟa uyirkaḷ ūṟu viḷaiyātapaṭiyum, koṉṟu tiṉṟuviṭātapaṭiyum pātukāttuk koḷḷutal. ivvāḻkkaip pōrāṭ ṭattil mikac cuṟucuṟuppum vaṉmaiyum uṭaiyaṉavē nilaipeṟukiṉṟaṉa. maṟṟavai maṭintu pōkiṉṟaṉa.
takutiyuṭaiyaṉa nilaipeṟutal
ellā uyirkaḷiṭattum tampōṉṟa cantatikaḷai uṇṭu paṇṇum iyaṟkai vaṉmai uṇṭu. vāḻkkaip pōrāṭṭattil takutipeṟṟu nilai peṟukiṉṟa oru peṇ taṉṉaippōṉṟa takutiyuṭaiya cantatiyaip perukkum poruṭṭu oru āṇait terintukoḷkiṟāḷ. ivvāṟu 
oru peṇ āṇait terintu koḷvatu iyaṟkait terivu eṉappaṭum. ivvāṟu takutiyuṭaiya oru āṇum oru peṇṇum cērntu tōṟṟu vikkum cantatikaḷ, ivvulakil vāḻkkaip pōrāṭṭattil veṟṟi yaṭaintu nīṇṭakālam vāḻntu pala cantatikaḷait tōṟṟuvikkum vaṉmai yuṭaiyaṉavākiṉṟaṉa.
iyaṟkait terivu
oru peṇ oru āṇait terintu koḷvatē iyaṟkai viti. āṇ, peṇṇait terintu koḷvatu iyaṟkai vitiyaṉṟu. peṇ vilaṅku oṉṟu karukkoḷḷum kālattil pala āṇ vilaṅkukaḷ ataṉai aṭaiya muntukiṉṟaṉa. appoḻutu avaṟṟuḷ pōṭṭi uṇṭākip pōr mūḷkiṉṟatu. avaṟṟuḷ iṟutiyāka veṟṟiyaṭaiyum vīramuṭaiya āṇ vilaṅkaiyē peṇ vilaṅku aṭaikiṉṟatu. peṇ vilaṅku pōriṟ kalantu koḷvatillai. atu, āṇkaḷukkiṭaiyil naṭakkum pōrai ayalē niṉṟu nōkkum. tārviṉ eṉṉum iyaṟkai nūṟpulavar oru peṇ māṉukkākap pala nāṭkaḷ pōrāṭiya pala kalaimāṉkaḷaip paṟṟi yum, iṟutiyil veṟṟiyaṭainta kalaimāṉaip peṇmāṉ aṭaintataip paṟṟiyum kūṟiyirukkiṉṟār. peṇ vilaṅkukaḷ karukkoḷḷum paruvakālattil, āṇ nāvippūṉai, puṉuku pūṉai eṉṉum vilaṅkukaḷ peṇ iṉaṅkaḷaik kavarum poruṭṭu naṟumaṇattaik kakkukiṉṟaṉa. pēṭumuṭṭaiyiṭṭuk kuñcu porikkum kālattil cila āṇ paṟavaikaḷiṉ niṟam mikavum miṉumiṉuppum aḻakum aṭaikiṉṟaṉa. ivai peṇṇiṉaṅ kaḷukku āṇ iṉaṅkaḷ kavarcci aḷikkumpaṭi avaikaḷukku iyaṟkai aḷittuḷḷa cila paṇpukaḷākum.

cuyamvaram
muṟkālattil peṇkaḷum ivvāṟē ceytārkaḷ. peṇ oruvaḷ maṇapparuvam aṭainta pōtu, avaḷ mālaiyiṭṭu oruvaṉaik kaṇava ṉākat teriyavirukkum ceyti, iḷam āṭavarukku aṟivikkappaṭṭatu. kuṟikkappaṭṭa nāḷil avarkaḷ peṇ vīṭṭil kūṭiṉārkaḷ. avarkaḷuḷ, yātum oru vīracceyal kāṇpippavaṉai avaḷ mālaiyiṭṭut terintu koṇṭāḷ. eppoḻutum āṭavar, peṇkaḷ tammai vīrareṉṟu matikkak kūṭiya ceyalkaḷaiyē ceyya virumpukiṉṟaṉar. muṟkālap pōrkaḷ perum pālum peṇkaḷ kāraṇamāka nērntaṉa eṉappaṭutal mikavum karuttiṟ koḷḷattakkatu. iṉṟu ulakil naṭakkum periya kuṟṟaṅkaḷ pala, āṭavar peṇkaḷai makiḻvippatu kāraṇamākac ceyyappaṭuvaṉavēyākum.

vilaṅkukaḷiṉ vāḻkkai
nīṇṭakālam aṭaikāttuk kuñcu porikkum paṟavaikaḷallāta piṟa uyirkaḷil āṇkaḷum peṇkaḷum cōṭikaḷāka vāḻvatillai. peṇkaḷ taṉiyē oru kūṭṭamākavum, āṇkaḷ taṉiyē maṟṟoru kūṭṭa mākavum vāḻkiṉṟaṉa. peṇ vilaṅkukaḷ ‘karukkoḷḷum’ kālattil māttiram peṇ vilaṅkukaḷiṭaiyē āṇ vilaṅkukaḷ kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. karuk koṇṭa piṉ peṇ vilaṅkukaḷ orupōtum āṇ vilaṅkukaḷait tammai aṇuka aṉumatippatillai.

ātikāla makkaḷiṉ vāḻkkai
makkaḷ vilaṅkiṉattaic cērntavarkaḷē. ākavē, avarkaḷum ātikāla vāḻkkaiyil vilaṅkukaḷaip pōlavē iruntārkaḷ. peṇkaḷ oru kūṭṭamāka vāḻntārkaḷ. peṇkaḷ karukkoḷḷum kālaṅ kaḷil iyaṟkaitterivu ceytu koṇṭārkaḷ. vilaṅkukaḷ, paṟavaikaḷ mutaliyavaikaḷukkup paruvakālaṅkaḷiṉ veppataṭpa nilai, atika uṇavu kiṭaittal pōṉṟa kāraṇaṅkaḷiṉāl āṇṭiṉ ciṟcila kālaṅ kaḷilēyē karukkoḷḷum vēṭkai tōṉṟum. ivvitiyē makkaḷuk kum uṇṭāyiruntatu. peṇkaḷ kūṭṭamāka ōriṭattil taṅkuvōrākavum, āṇkaḷ vēṭṭaiyāṭuvōrākavum iruntaṉar.



tāyāṭci
muṟkālattil kuḻantaikaḷ tāy vaḻiyākavē aṟiyappaṭṭārkaḷ. peṇkaḷ ōriṭattil iruntamaiyāl paḻamaraṅkaḷai vaittu uṇṭākki yum, tāṉiyaṅkaḷaip payiriṭṭum uṇavupporuḷkaḷai uṇṭākki ṉārkaḷ. *peṇkaḷ kūṭṭattukku vayatil mūtta tāyē talaiviyākavirun tāḷ. maṟṟavarkaḷ avaḷukku aṭaṅki naṭantārkaḷ. ivvāṟu ulakil tāyāṭci uṇṭākiyiruntatu. cottu peṇkaḷukkē uriyatāyiruntatu. ak kālattil tantaikkup piḷḷaikaḷiṭattil urimai uṇṭākavillai.

contak kūṭṭattil maṇam illai
oru kūṭṭattil tōṉṟiya peṇkaḷum āṇkaḷum cakōtara cakōtarikaḷākak karutappaṭṭaṉar. oru kūṭṭattiluḷḷa peṇkaḷ iṉṉoru tāy vaṭṭattiluḷḷa āṇkaḷaic cērntu piḷḷaikaḷaip peṟṟārkaḷ. ulakil ellāp pākattilum contak kūṭṭattil peṇ koḷḷutal kūṭātu eṉṉum caṭṭam iruntu vantatu. iṉṟum piṟpōk kuḷḷa makkaḷiṭaiyē iccaṭṭam kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. intiya nāṭṭilum pala kūṭṭattiṉar tamatu contak kōttirattil peṇ koḷvatillai. ivaiyellām tāyāṭci uṇṭāyirunta kālattil tōṉṟiyirunta vaḻakkaṅkaḷ.

tāyk kaṭavuḷ
ulakil mikap paḻamaiyākat tōṉṟiyatu tāy vaḻipāṭu. tāy ellā atikāramum uṭaiyavaḷāka iruntāḷ. piḷḷaikaḷ avaḷaic cūḻa iruntārkaḷ. avarkaḷ kaṭavuḷai ellā atikāramumuṭaiya, tāy vaṭivil vaḻipaṭṭārkaḷ. ivvulakam muḻuvatilum tāykkaṭavuḷiṉ vaḻipāṭu orē vakaiyākak kāṇappaṭṭatu. tāy kaṉṉi eṉappaṭṭāḷ. nilaiyāṉa maṇak kaṭṭuppāṭu illāmaiyāl, tāy piḷḷaikaḷaip peṟṟa pōtum, kaṉṉi yākavē karutappaṭṭāḷ. ulaka muḻumaiyilum tāyk kaṭavuḷ kaṉṉik kaṭavuḷākavē koḷḷappaṭṭatu.

nākarikam peṇkaḷiṭaiyē tōṉṟi vaḷarntatu
peṇkaḷ uṇavup poruḷkaḷai viḷaivittārkaḷ eṉa muṉ kūṟiṉōm. avarkaḷ ōriṭattil taṅkiyiruntapōtu, peṭṭi, kaṭakam, kūṭai muṭaital pōṉṟa palavakaik kaittoḻilkaḷaic ceytārkaḷ; paruttiyai uṇṭākkip pañciliruntu nūl nūṟṟu āṭai neytārkaḷ. avarkaḷ tām aṟinta poruḷkaḷaik kuṟikka ovvōr olikkuṟikaḷai iṭṭu avaikaḷait tam kuḻantaikaḷukkum kaṟpittārkaḷ. ivvāṟu peṇkaḷiṭaiyē payirttoḻil, kaittoḻil mutaliyavaiyum, moḻiyum tōṉṟi vaḷarcciyaṭaintaṉa. āṭavar vēṭṭaiyāṭuvōrāka vum, cila camayaṅkaḷil uṇavukkup peṇkaḷaiyē etirpārppavarkaḷāka vum iruntaṉar. makkaḷ muṟparuvattil vilaṅkukaḷai oppa oruvarōṭu oruvar pōrāṭik koṇṭiruntārkaḷ. taṟkāppiṉ poruṭṭē perum pālum makkaḷ oṉṟu cērntu vāḻntārkaḷ. oru kūṭṭattiṉar iṉṉoru kūṭṭattiṉarait tākkumpōtu avarkaḷai etirttup pōrāṭutal pōṉṟa ceyalukku māttiram āṇkaḷ payaṉpaṭṭārkaḷ.

kuḻantaikaḷukkut tāyē ciṟantavaḷ
kuḻantaikaḷ utaviyaṟṟavarkaḷākap piṟakkiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ tāmākat tamatu vāḻkkaiyai naṭattat takutiyaṟṟavarkaḷāyirukkiṉṟārkaḷ. appoḻutu tāyē kuḻantaikaḷukkup pālūṭṭi vaḷarkkiṉṟāḷ. atu, veḷi ulakil ceṉṟu taṉatu vāḻkkaiyait tāṉē naṭattum takuti peṟumvaraiyum tāyiṉ ātaravil irukkiṉṟatu. tāy, kuḻantaikaḷiṭat til vaikkum paṟṟu mikap puṉitamāṉatu. kuḻantaikaḷiṭattil tāy vaippa tākiya paṟṟuk kīḻ iṉa uyirkaḷ mutal, mēl iṉa uyirkaḷ varaiyiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. cila iṉappaṟavaikaḷ tamatu kuñcukaḷaik kāppataṟ kākat tamatu uyiraiyum viṭukiṉṟaṉa. vilaṅkiṉaṅkaḷum appaṭiyē. tāy pālūṭṭi vaḷarkkum vilaṅkiṉaṅkaḷ kuṭṭikaḷ mītu vaikkum paṟṟu mika mēlāṉatu. kuraṅkukaḷiṭattilum maṉitariṭattum ippaṟṟu aḷavu kaṭantu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. kuraṅkukkuṭṭi iṟantu viṭṭāl, ataṉ tāy iraṇṭu mūṉṟu nāṭkaḷukku ataṉai uṭaṉ koṇṭu cellum; mikak kavalai yōṭu amaitiyāka irukkum; uṇavu koḷḷamāṭṭātu.

tāyaṉpu
tāy kuḻantaiyiṭattil vaikkum aṉpu mēlāṉatu. tāy patil palaṉai etirpārātu kuḻantaiyiṭattē aṉpu vaikkiṉṟāḷ. atu makiḻvataip pārttu avaḷum makiḻkiṉṟāḷ; atu varuntuvataik kaṇṭu tāṉum varuntukiṉṟāḷ. patil palaṉai etirpārātu celuttum aṉpu kaṭavuḷ taṉmaiyuṭaiyatu. taṉṉalam karutātu ivvulakiṟ cilavaṟṟaic ceykiṉṟavarkaḷaip puṉitamuṭaiyavarkaḷāka vaittu ulakam pōṟṟukiṉ ṟatu. “taṉakkeṉa vāḻāp piṟarkkuriyāḷaṉ” eṉṉum uyarnta paṇpu kāraṇamākap puttar ulaka makkaḷāl pōṟṟappaṭṭār. kiṟittunātar, mukammatu napi, koṉpiyūcas, makāvīrar mutaliyavarkaḷait teyvaṅkaḷāka makkaḷ vaṇaṅkip pōṟṟuvatu avarkaḷ piṟarukkāka vāḻntamaiyiṉālē yākum. iṉṟu kānti, makāttumā eṉap pōṟṟappaṭuvatum ik karuttup paṟṟiyē. ik koḷkaip paṟṟiyē, ovvoru tāyum taṉ kuḻantai kaḷukkut teyvam āvāḷ. “aṉṉaiyum pitāvum muṉṉaṟi teyvam” eṉṟatum ik karuttup paṟṟiyatēyākum. kaṭavuḷ ovvoru vīṭṭilum iruppataṟkup patil, tāyai aṅku aṉuppiyuḷḷār eṉa oru aṟiñar kūṟiyuḷḷār.

tantai aṉpu
paṟavai, vilaṅkukaḷ, tantai aṉpaip paṟṟi aṟiyā. avai kaḷukkut tāyaṉpu oṉṟē uḷḷatu. kuḻantai vaḷarntu veḷiyulakattai aṟiya varukiṉṟatu. appoḻutu kuḻantaikku vēṇṭiya piṟa utavikaḷait tantai aḷikkiṟāṉ. ataṉāl kuḻantai tantaiyiṭattil aṉpu koḷkiṉṟatu. āyiṉum, atu tantaiyiṭattil koḷḷumaṉpu tāyiṭattuk koṇṭuḷḷa aṉpilum aḷaviṟ kuṟaivuṭaiyatē.

peṇkaḷiṭattil meṉmaik kuṇam
ellā āṇ iṉaṅkaḷum vaṉmaik kuṇam uṭaiyaṉavākavum peṇ iṉaṅkaḷ meṉmaik kuṇam uṭaiyaṉavākavum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ciṅkam, puli pōṉṟa mūrkka kuṇamuṭaiya vilaṅkiṉaṅkaḷil peṇ iṉaṅ kaḷai viṭa āṇ iṉaṅkaḷē pōr ceyyum atika mūrkkak kuṇamuṭai yaṉavākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. paṭaippukkaḷuḷ makkaṭpaṭaippu iṟuti yāṉatu eṉṟu ōr iṭattiṟ kūṟiyuḷḷōm. ātiyil makkaḷukku vilaṅ kiṉaṅkaḷaip pōṉṟu oruvarōṭu oruvar etirttup pōrceyyum mūrkkak kuṇam uṭaiyavarkaḷākavē iruntaṉar. makkaḷ tōṉṟi eṇṇillāta kālam ivvulakil vāḻntu varukiṉṟamaiyāl avarkaḷiṉ vilaṅkuk kuṇaṅkaḷ paṭippaṭiyē taṇivu paṭṭup paṇpaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avvāṟāyiṉum peṇkaḷai viṭa āṇkaḷē muraṭṭuttaṉam uṭaiyavarkaḷākak kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉar. āṇkaḷiṉ mūrkkak kuṇam taṇivupaṭṭup paṇpaṭaitaṟkuk kāraṇarāyiruntavarkaḷ peṇkaḷēyāvar. peṇkaḷ tāymārākum pōtu, avarkaḷ uḷḷattil aṉpu tōṉṟip perukukiṉṟatu. tāymār kuḻantai kaḷukku aṉpōṭu pāl ūṭṭi vaḷarttaliṉ aṉpuk kuṇaṅkaḷ avar māṭṭum cuvaṟukiṉṟaṉa. ivvāṟu talaimuṟai talaimuṟaiyākat tāymār tamatu aṉpuk kuṇaṅkaḷaip piḷḷaikaḷ mūlam parapputaliṉ āṇkaḷum paṭippaṭiyē paḻaiya muraṭṭut taṉmaiyiṉiṉṟum nīṅki, aṉpu ceṟinta uḷḷattavarkaḷāka vantuḷḷārkaḷ. makkaḷ uḷḷattiṉ iyalpukaḷai naṉku ārāynta aṟiñarkaḷ peṇkaḷē āṭavaraic cīrtiruttiyavar kaḷāvar eṉak kūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ. aṉpukku iruppiṭam peṇṇē. peṇ ṇiṭamiruntē aṉpu veḷḷam ūṟṟeṭuttu āṇ pālārkaḷiṭaiyē yum paravuvatāyiṟṟu.

peṇkaḷ uyarvaṭaintirunta pōtu nākarikam ōṅkiyiruntatu
iṉṟaikku aiyāyiram allatu āṟāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ, intiyā, mēṟku āciyā, ekiptu, kirēttā (Crete) mutaliya nāṭukaḷ nāka rikattil mika ōṅkiyiruntaṉa. in nākarikaṅkaḷiṉ uṟaḻcci piṟaḻccikaḷē iṉṟaiya nākarikaṅkaḷākum. ulaka nākarikaṅkaḷuḷ mikappaḻamaiyuṭaiyatu cintu veḷi nākarikam eṉap pērāciriyar piṟāṅpōṭ eṉṉum aṟiñar kūṟukiṉṟār. moñcatarō nākarika kālattil tāyāṭciyē iruntatu eṉa, “intiyāvil tāyāṭci” eṉṉum nūl eḻutiya paraṉ ōmar ralv ernels eṉpār kūṟiyuḷḷār. ekiptiya peṇkaḷ ellā nāṭṭup peṇkaḷaiyum viṭa mēlāṉa nilai eytiyiruntārkaḷ. ekiptiyap peṇkaḷaip pōla vēṟenta nāṭṭup peṇkaḷum atikāram peṟṟu viḷaṅkavillai eṉa hāl eṉpār kūṟiyuḷḷār. nail āṟṟai aṭutta nāṭukaḷiluḷḷa makkaḷaippōla vēṟenta nāṭṭiṉarum peṇkaḷukku avvaḷavu uyarvu koṭukkavillai eṉa māksmullar kūṟiyuḷḷār. maṇamāṉa allatu maṇamākāta entap peṇṇum, taṉatu viruppiṉpaṭi evvakaik koṭukkal vāṅkalum ceyyakkūṭiyavaḷākaviruntāḷ. avarkaḷaik kaṇavaṉō, tāy tantaiyarō, tamaiyaṉ tampimārō kaṭṭuppaṭutta urimaiyillātavarkaḷā yiruntaṉar. peṇṇiṉ peyarāl kaṇavaṉ pattiraṅkaḷ eḻutavō koṭukkal vāṅkal ceyyavō atikāram peṟṟirukkavillai. āṉāl peṇ kaṇavaṉiṉ cārpil avvāṟu ceyya atikāram peṟṟiruntāḷ. mūṉṟām irāmcē eṉṉum aracaṉ kālattup paṭṭaiyamoṉṟil “peṇ, tāṉ virumpiya evarōṭāvatu cellalām. avaḷai oruvarum kaṭṭuppaṭutta muṭiyātu” eṉa eḻutappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu. ekiptilē peṇkaḷ kaṭait teruvukkuc ceṉṟu vāṇikam ceytārkaḷ. avarkaḷiṉ kaṇavar vīṭṭiliruntu āṭai neytārkaḷ eṉṟu heratōttacu kūṟiyuḷḷār. eḻuttu vēlaiyil ārampitta oru peṇ, paṭippaṭiyē pataviyil uyarntu, oru nāṭṭukku āṭci atikāriyākip (Governor) piṉpu irāṇuva atikāriyāka vanta varalāṟu oṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.

arēpiyāvilē peṇkaḷē paṇattukkuc contakkārarāyirun taṉar; avarkaḷiṭattil periya mantaik kūṭṭaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. avarkaḷiṉ kaṇavar mantaikaḷai mēyppōrāyiruntaṉar. kaliyāṇam nīkkuvatiṉ aṭaiyāḷam kaṇavaṉ “iṉi nāṉ mantaikaḷai mēyccal nilattukku ōṭṭic cellamāṭṭēṉ” eṉak kūṟuvatākum.

ekiptilē piḷḷaikaḷ tāykkē contamuṭaiyavarkaḷ. kaṇavaṉum maṉaiviyum pirintu vāḻa nērntāl piḷḷaikaḷ tāyōṭu taṅkiṉar. ekiptiya kuṭumpaṅkaḷ tāyāṭci muṟaiyāṉa amaippuṭaiyaṉa. oru kuṭum pattil peṇṇai maṇappavaṉ kuṭumpattil mutaṉmai peṟavillai. ekiptiyak kīḻ vakuppu, naṭuttara vakuppup peṇkaḷ tirumaṇattukkuppiṉ cutantiramuṭaiyavarkaḷākavum, mariyātaik kuriyavarkaḷākavum iruntārkaḷ. maṉaivi eṉṉum muṟaiyil avaḷē vīṭṭukku araci (illāḷ). vīṭṭilē mutaṉmaiyuṭaiyavar tantaiyallar; tāyē. kuṭumpattil kaṇavaṉait talaimaiyākak kūṟum caṭṭam etuvum ekiptil irukka villai. pillē pērar eṉṉum aracaṉ kālattiṟkup piṉpē, kaṇavaṉ eṉṉum poruḷai uṇarttum col ākkappaṭṭatu. āṭavaṉ, vīṭṭil taṅkupavaṉākavum cottuc cutantaraṅkaḷ ellām peṇṇukkē uriyaṉa vākavum koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. oruvaṉ putiya vīṭu oṉṟaik kaṭṭiṉāl atu, maṉaiviyiṉ kaikku māṟiṟṟu. maṇamāṉa peṇkaḷum, maṇamākāta peṇ kaḷum taṅkaḷ cottait tāmē āṇṭu aṉupavittārkaḷ. maṉaiviyiṉ karumaṅkaḷaip paṟṟik kaṇavaṉ oṉṟum aṟiyātavaṉāyiruntāṉ. kaṇavaṉ, maṉaiviyiṉ cottil talaiyiṭuvatu caṭṭa cammatamillātatākak koḷḷappaṭṭatu. vayatāṉa peṟṟōraik kāppaṟṟum poṟuppup peṇ makkaḷiṉ kaṭamaiyākaviruntatu. tirumaṇaṅkaḷ camaya campantamāṉa kiriyaikaḷōṭu naṭattappaṭavillai.

kirēttāvil (Crete) cottu peṇkaḷ vacamē iruntatu. kaṇavaṉukkuc cottil talaiyiṭum atikāram ciṟitum irukkavillai. piḷḷaikaḷ tāy tantai eṉṉum iruvariṉ cottukkaḷaiyum peṟṟārkaḷ. virumpiṉāl kaliyāṇattai nīkkik koḷḷum atikāram peṇṇukku iruntatu.

ivvāṟu muṟkālattil nākarikattāṟ ciṟantu viḷaṅkiya nāṭukaḷi lellām, peṇkaḷē mutal iṭam peṟṟiruntārkaḷ eṉa aṟikiṉṟōm. mauriya cantirakuptaṉiṉ araṇmaṉaiyil tūtarākavirunta mekastaṉīs eṉṉum kirēkkar pāṇṭiya nāṭu peṇkaḷālāḷappaṭukiṟateṉak kūṟi yirukkiṉṟār. itaṉāl tamiḻ nāṭṭil paḻaṅkālattil cotturimai peṟ ṟiruntavarkaḷ peṇkaḷē eṉat teriya varukiṉṟatu. oru āyiram āṇṭu kaḷukku muṉ, mēṟku nāṭukaḷ mikap piṟpōkkāṉa nilaiyil iruntaṉa. mēṟku nāṭukaḷil peṇkaḷ mutaṉmai aṭaiyat toṭaṅkiyatum, aṅku nākarikam paṭippaṭiyē vaḷarcciyaṭaintu iṉṟaiya nilaiyai aṭaintu viṭṭatu. peṇkaḷukku mutaṉmai koṭāta kiḻakku nāṭukaḷil nākarikam iruḷaṭaintu kiṭakkiṉṟatu. makkaḷ mikavum kīḻnilaiyil irukkiṉṟaṉar.

tantai āṭci
ulakil āṭci eṉpatu valimaiyaiyum celvanilaiyaiyum aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭatu. makkaḷ vāḻkkaiyiṉ muṟ paruvattup peṇkaḷē nākarikattukku vēṇṭiya aṭippaṭaiyākavuḷḷa palavakait toḻilkaḷaiyum ārampittu vaittārkaḷ. muṟkāla makkaḷiṉ celvam eṉpatu uṇavē. evaṉiṭattu atika uṇavup poruḷuṇṭō avaṉē celvaṉākak karutappaṭṭāṉ. toṭakkattil peṇkaḷiṭamē atika uṇavup poruḷkaḷ iruntaṉa. ākavē, avarkaḷ celvarkaḷāka viruntaṉar. āṭavar avarkaḷ ēviyavaṟṟaic ceytum, pōrkkālattil pakaivarōṭu pōr ceytu peṇkaḷaip pātukāttum vantaṉar. ēr pūṭṭi uḻutu atika tāṉiyam viḷaivikkum muṟai aṟiyappaṭṭapōtu, vēṭar kaḷāka alaintu tirinta āṭavar palar, tāṉiyaṅkaḷai viḷaivikkat toṭaṅkiṉar. ituvaraiyum peṇkaḷ ceytuvanta payirt toḻilai avarkaḷ kaippaṟṟiṉar. ataṉāl celvanilai peṇkaḷ kaiyiliruntu āṭavar kaikku māṟat toṭaṅkiṟṟu. āṭavaṉ ciṟitu ciṟitākac cottukkut talaivaṉāṉāṉ. avvāṟu vantapōtum peṇ āṭcik kālattilirunta pala vaḻakkaṅkaḷum iruntu vantaṉa. peṇ āṭcik kālattil peṇṇuk kup pūraṇa cutantaram iruntatu. avaḷ virumpiya oruvaṉaik kaṇavaṉākat terintu koḷḷalām. vēṇṭātapōtu avaṉai viṭṭu vēṟoru vaṉaik kaṇavaṉākat terintu koḷḷalām. tantai āṭci uṇṭāṉa piṉpu, āṭavar ciṟitu ciṟitākap peṇkaḷiṉ cutantirattaik kaṭṭuppaṭuttat toṭaṅkiṉārkaḷ. oru peṇ oruvaṉai maṇantāl, avaḷ taṉatu vāḻkkaik kālam muḻumaiyilum avaṉuṭaiya maṉaiviyāka viruttal vēṇṭumeṉṉum caṭṭaṅkaḷum camūka vaḻakkak kaṭṭuppāṭukaḷum uṇṭāyiṉa. tantaiyāṭci uṇṭāṉa piṉpē, nilaiyāṉa tirumaṇaṅkaḷ ulakil tōṉṟi valivaṭaintaṉa.

peṇkaḷiṉ uyar nilaiyum tāḻ nilaiyum
tāy āṭci muṟaiyaip piṉpaṟṟi nākarika kālattil peṇkaḷ uyar nilaiyil iruntārkaḷ. makkaḷ mūrkkamāṉa vilaṅkukaḷaippōla oru kūṭṭattiṉarōṭu iṉṉoru kūṭṭattiṉar pōr ceytal pōṉṟa muraṭṭuk kuṇaṅkaḷuṭaiyavarkaḷāyiruntaṉar. iṉṟum makkaḷ pōrk kuṇamuṭaiyavarkaḷākavē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. ak kālattil oru kūṭṭattiṉar iṉṉoru kūṭṭattiṉarait tākki avarkaḷai veṉṟāl, avarkaḷ āṭavar ellōraiyum koṉṟuviṭṭup peṇkaḷai aṭimai kaḷākak koṇṭu celvatu vaḻakkam. ivvāṟu aṭimaikaḷākac ceṉṟa peṇkaḷ pakaivarkaḷiṭam māṭukaḷ kutiraikaḷpōla uḻaittārkaḷ. avarkaḷ pakaivaraic cērntu piḷḷaikaḷaip peṟṟup pakaivarkaḷiṭai yum tamatu irattak kalappai uṇṭākkiṉārkaḷ. ulakam muḻumaiyilum contak kūṭṭattiṉaruḷ peṇkoḷḷutal ākātu eṉṉum vaḻakku iruntamaiyiṉ, oru kūṭṭattavaṉ iṉṉoru kūṭṭattiliruntu peṇṇait tiruṭikkoṇṭu celvatum vaḻakkāka iruntatu. itaṉāl paḻaṅkāla makkaḷiṭaiyē peṇkaḷait tiruṭi maṇam purintukoḷva tākiya vaḻakkamum valivaṭaintiruntatu.

tirumaṇattiṉ carittiram
mika muṟkālattil tirumaṇam irukkavillai; atu iṉṉateṉṟum aṟiyappaṭavillai. āṭavaṉ oruvaṉ peṇ oruttiyōṭu naṭpāyirun tāl, ataṉāl avaṉ caṭṭapūrvamākavō camaya pūrvamākavō kaṭṭuppaṭa villai. āṭavarum makaḷirum tattam viruppappaṭi vāḻum urimaiyaip peṟṟiruntaṉar. āṭavar peṇkaḷai valimaiyāṟ piṭittu maṇañceytu koḷḷum kālam varaiyil nilaimai ivvāṟē iruntatu. peṇṇai valimaiyāṟ piṭippatu, eṉpatu uṇmaiyāka allatu pōliyākavirukka lām. oru peṇṇai valimaiyāṟ piṭippatāl, avaṉukku oru aṭimai kiṭaikkiṉṟatu. itaṉāl contak kūṭṭattil peṇ koḷvataṟkup patil ayaṟ kūṭṭattil peṇ koḷḷum vaḻakku uṇṭāyiṟṟu. ivvakai maṇaṅkaḷāl iraṇṭu kūṭṭattiṉarukkum iṇaippu uṇṭāyiṟṟu. ataṉāl avarkaḷ valimaipeṟṟut tammaip pakaivariṭamiruntu kāppāṟṟik koḷḷat takkatākaviruntatu. piṟkālaṅkaḷil iru kūṭṭaṅ kaḷilum ciṟuvarukku maṇamceytu iṇaippukkaḷ uṇṭākkappaṭṭaṉa.

peṇkaḷaik kavarum vaḻakkattōṭu, kavarvataṟkup patil peṇ kaḷai vilaikoṭuttu vāṅkum vaḻakkam uṇṭāyiṟṟu. tiruntāta makka ḷiṭaiyē iv vaḻakku nīṇṭa kālam toṭarntu varuvatāyiṟṟu. ak kālaṅ kaḷil peṇ, tantaiyōṭu iruntāḷ. tāṉ virumpiya evaṉukkāvatu avaḷai atika vilaikku viṟṟuviṭum urimaiyiruntatu. viṟpavaṉ, vāṅku pavaṉiṉ naṟkuṇattukkallāmal, poruḷiṉ tokaikkākavē avaṉai virumpiṉāṉ. iv vaḻakkam irukkum kālattil peṇṇukku avaḷ virumpiya oruvaṉait terinteṭukkum urimai irukkavillai. tantai allatu avaḷiṉ pātukāppukkuriyavaṉ terinteṭukkum oruvaṉukku avaḷ viṟkappaṭalāṉāḷ. avaḷ avaṉuṭaiya aṭimaiyāṉāḷ.

ik koḷkai niyāyamāṉataṉṟu eṉṟum, paṇattukkākap peṇ kaḷai aṭimaikaḷ ākkutal kūṭātu eṉṟum, makkaḷ kālappōkkil uṇarat toṭaṅkiṉārkaḷ. appoḻutu peṇṇai vilaikoṭuttu vāṅkupavaṉ, allatu kaṇavaṉ, peṇṇiṉ tantaiyiṭam cītaṉam kēṭṭāṉ. immuṟaiyiṉāl peṇṇai vilai koṭuttu vāṅkum vaḻakkam oḻintatu. vilai koṭuttu vāṅkiya vaḻakkattiṉ niḻalē iṉṟu pariyam eṉṟum peyar peṟṟu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. pariyam koṭuppatu eṉpatu pōliyāka vāṅkuvataik kuṟikkiṉṟatu.



itaṉāl peṇṇukkuk koṭukkappaṭum paṇam peṇṇiṉ vilai aḷavil irāmal, peyar maṭṭil ciṟu tokaiyākaviruntatu; viṟpavaṉ vāṅkupavaṉukku allatu peṇṇukku vilaipporuḷil oru pakutiyaik koṭuttāṉ. ataṉpiṉ, tantai peṇ vaḻiyākak kaṇavaṉukkup poruḷ kiṭaikkumpaṭi cītaṉam aḷittāṉ. innilaiyilum peṇṇukkuk kaṇavaṉait teriyum urimai irukkavillai; tantaiyē avaḷukkuk kaṇavaṉait terintāṉ. itaṉpiṉ peṇṇai vilai koṭuttu vāṅkum vaḻakkam maṟaintu pōyiṟṟu. taṉatu peṇṇukkut takunta kaṇavaṉait tēṭa vēṇṭum eṉṉum eṇṇam tantaikku iruntamaiyāl, avaṉ peṇṇukkēṟṟa kaṇavaṉait tēṭavēṇṭiyiruntatu. itaṉpiṉ peṇ, tāṉ virumpiya kaṇavaṉait terintu koḷḷum nilai uṇṭāyiṟṟu. itu vaḷarcciyaṭaintapōtu purōkitar maṇaṅkaḷaik kiriyaikaḷōṭu naṭattivaittaṉar. peṇkaḷiṉ nilai oru kālattil uyarvu peṟṟiruntu, piṉ tāḻvu nilai aṭaintu, maṟupaṭiyum nākarika kālattil uyarvaṭainta teṉa aṟikiṉṟōm.

iṭaikkālattil peṇkaḷ nilai vīḻcciyuṟṟataṟkuk kāraṇam
ivvulakam, vāḻkkaip pōrāṭṭam, takutiyuṭaiyatu nilai peṟutal, eṉṉum iraṇṭu iyaṟkai vitikaḷukku uṭpaṭṭuc celkiṉṟatu. valiyatu meliyataik koṉṟu tiṉṉutalai allatu valiyatu meliyatai aṭakki āḷutalaik kāṇkiṉṟōm. vāḻkkaip pōrāṭṭattil “valimaiyē niyāyam”. iṉṟu valiya cātiyār meliya cātiyārai aṭakki āḷvatum ivviti paṟṟiyē. makkaḷ tiruntāta kālattil avarkaḷiṉ uṇarcci vilaṅkukaḷiṉ uṇarcci pōlavē iruntatu. āṇ peṇ eṉṉum iru pālāril peṇkaḷaiviṭa āṇkaḷē uṭal valiyilum muraṭṭuk kuṇattilum mikuntiruntārkaḷ. ākavē, avarkaḷ tammiṉum melinta peṇkaḷai aṭakkit tamatu vāḻkkaip pōrāṭṭattukkup payaṉpaṭuttik koṇṭārkaḷ. makkaḷiṉ uḷḷam paṇpaṭṭu aṟivu ōṅkiya kālattil, avarkaḷiṭaiyē niyāya uṇarcci tōṉṟiṟṟu. ak kālaṅ kaḷil avarkaḷ āṭavaraiviṭap peṇkaḷē ulakukkup payaṉuḷḷavarkaḷ eṉa uṇarntārkaḷ. appoḻutu peṇkaḷ paṭippaṭiyē ellā atikāraṅkaḷaiyum peṟṟu viḷaṅkiṉārkaḷ.

kirēkkar urōmarkaḷiṉ tirumaṇam
kirēkka nāṭṭil tirumaṇam taṉippaṭṭa urimaiyuṭaiyatu māttiramallāmal, aracāṅkattōṭu campantappaṭṭatākavum iruntatu. ispāṭṭāvil, itaṉ toṭarpāṉa caṭṭaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. ilaikarkas (Lycurgus) eṉpavariṉ caṭṭappaṭi uriyakālattil maṇañceyyātavar mītum, mikavum kālañ ceṉṟu maṇañ ceypavarkaḷ mītum, icaivillāta maṇañ ceytavarkaḷ mītum vaḻakkut toṭarakkūṭiyatākac caṭṭam iruntatu. ovvoruvarum nalla kuḻantaikaḷaip peṟṟu nāṭṭukku aḷikka vēṇṭumeṉpatē akkālak koḷkai. maṇañceyvatu kuḻantaikaḷaip peṟuvataṟku māttiram eṉṟu karutappaṭṭatu. oru peṇ taṉ kaṇavaṉ mūlam kuḻantaikaḷaip peṟa iyalātavaḷāyiruntāl, avaḷ iṉṉoru vaṉaic cēralām eṉṟu māttiramalla, cēravēṇṭumeṉṟu caṭṭam kaṭṭāyappaṭuttiṟṟu. piḷāṟṟō eṉpavariṉ caṭṭappaṭi, muppattaintu vayatukku muṉ maṇam ceytu koḷḷātavar kaṭumaiyākat taṇṭikkappaṭa lām. peṇṇai maṉaiviyākat terivatu nāṭṭiṉ nalaṉaikkarutiyē yaṉṟic conta nalaṉ karutiyaṉṟu eṉa avar kūṟiyuḷḷār.



tirumaṇam puṉitamuṭaiyatu
tirumaṇam cantatikaḷait tōṟṟuvikkum nōkkamākat tōṉṟiyatu. ellā makkaḷum tirumaṇattaip puṉitamuṭaiyatākavē koṇṭu varukiṉṟaṉar. vilaṅkukaḷ, paṟavaikaḷ mutaliya kīḻ iṉa uyirkaḷ cantatiyait tōṟṟuvikkum kālattilaṉṟi āṇ peṇ cērkkai ceyva tillai. kīḻ nilaiyil uḷḷa makkaḷiṭaiyil ivvaḻakkē nilaipeṟu kiṉṟatu. makkaḷ nākarikam eṉṉum pōrvaiyil ceyaṟkaiyāka vāḻat toṭaṅkiyapōtu, āṇ peṇ cērkkaiyai iṉpattukkuriyatu eṉak koṇṭu atil aḻuntuvārāyiṉar. itaṟkākak kokkōkam pōṉṟa iṉpa nūlkaḷ eḻutappaṭalāyiṉa. annūlkaḷ eppaṭiyirukkiṉṟa teṉṟāl, ivvāṟu kaḻuttil curukkiṭṭuk koṇṭu (tūkkiliṭṭu) maṉitaṉ cākalām eṉpatupōla veṉka. maṉitaṉ āṇ peṇ cērkkaiyil celaviṭum āṟṟalait tiraṭṭi, vēṟu vaḻiyil celaviṭuvatāl periya kāriyaṅkaḷaic cātikkalām eṉak kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. caiṉa, pautta, kiṟittuvat tuṟavikaḷ kalvit toṭarpāka atikavēlai ceytataṟkuk kāraṇam ituvēyākum.

nāṭṭiṉ muṉṉēṟṟattukkut tāyē kāraṇam
piḷḷaikaḷiṉ vaḷarccikkum naṟ paḻakkaṅkaḷukkum tāyē poṟuppāka irukkiṉṟāḷ. avaḷ kuḻantaikaḷiṉ uṭal vaḷarcciyaik kavaṉippatu pōla, aṟivu vaḷarcciyaiyum kavaṉikkakkūṭiya nilaiyil iruppāḷēyāyiṉ avaḷ, kuḻantaikaḷiṉ maṉattaiyum māṟṟiviṭalām. iraṇṭu talaimuṟaikaḷil ivvulakil pōrkaḷ talaikāṭṭāmal oḻintuviṭum. vāḻkkaiyiṉ mattiya iṭam vīṭu. vīṭukaḷil tōṉṟum nākarikamē oru cātiyāriṉ nākarikamāka vaḷarum. oru cātiyār nākarikam aṭaintu muṉṉēṟuvataṟkut tāyē kāraṇam.

kiḻakkut tēcap peṇkaḷ
kīḻ nāṭṭup peṇkaḷum, mēl nāṭṭup peṇkaḷum talaimuṟai yāka varum paramparaip paḻakkaṅkaḷāṟ kaṭṭuppaṭṭirukkiṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷ nīṇṭakālamākat tamatu poruḷātārattukkum pātukāppukkum tam kaṇavaraiyē nampi varukiṉṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷ ivvāṟu otukkappaṭ ṭiruppatu mēl nāṭṭavarkaḷukku aṭimai vāḻvākat tōṉṟum. peṇ kaḷai aṟiyāmaiyil vaittiruppatāl pala tīmaikaḷ uṇṭākum. ekiptiyap peṇ taṉatu kuḻantaikkuk kaṇṇūṟu paṭātirukkumpaṭi ataṟkuk kantaikaḷai uṭuttirukkiṟāḷ. cīṉattāy, taṉatu āṇ kuḻantaiyai peṇ kuḻantaiyeṉṟu teyvaṅkaḷ niṉaikkumpaṭi ataṉ kātaik kuttivaḷaiyam iṭukiṟāḷ. avaḷ, camayac caṭṭaṅkaḷāl, kaṇavaṉ, tantai, cakōtaraṉ pōṉṟa āṭavarukku aṭaṅki naṭakkavum, avarkaḷaik kaṭavuḷāka niṉaittu naṭakkavum paḻakkappaṭṭuḷḷāḷ. ataṉāl avaḷ tāṉāka oṉṟai niṉaikkum āṟṟalai maṟantuviṭṭāḷ. avaḷaik kaṭṭuppaṭutti vaikka vēṇṭumeṉṟu camayac caṭṭam collukiṉṟatu. avaḷukkut taṉatu kuḻantaikaḷaiyum, kirāmap peṇkaḷ cilaraiyum, avarkaḷ vīṭukaḷaiyumē teriyum. avaḷ oṉṟaip paṟṟi yōcaṉai ceyyātirukkumpaṭi paḻakkappaṭṭuḷḷāḷ. ataṉāl avaḷ taṉṉāl niṉaikka muṭiyāteṉa nampukiṉṟāḷ. paḷḷikkūṭattil paṭittu veḷi yulakattai aṟinta peṇkaḷ tamatu kaṇavarāl vaṟpuṟuttappaṭum ivvakai vāḻkkaiyāl tuṉpuṟukiṉṟaṉar. ciṟaik kūṭattil iruppatākavē avarkaḷ uṇarukiṉṟaṉar. oru mirukattaik kaṭṭi aviḻppavaṉaip pōla, avaḷukku avaḷ kaṇavaṉ tōṉṟukiṉṟāṉ.




peṇkaḷ āṇkaḷukkuk kīḻppaṭital
toṭakkattil kaṇavaṉ maṉaiviyarukkuḷḷa kaṭamaikaḷ pirikkap paṭṭiruntaṉa. peṇ, vīṭṭiluḷḷa kaṭamaikaḷaic ceytāḷ. āṭavaṉ veḷiyiluḷḷa cila kaṭamaikaḷaip pārttu vantāṉ. itaṉāl peṇ tāḻntavaḷ, kaṇavaṉ uyarntavaṉ eṉṟāvatu, peṇ uyarntavaḷ, kaṇavaṉ tāḻntavaṉ eṉṟāvatu karuttu uṇṭākavillai. iruvarum camamāṉa varkaḷ eṉṉum karuttē uṇṭāyiruntatu.

muṟkālaṅkaḷil perumpālōr aṭimaikaḷākavē iruntaṉar. peṇkaḷum appaṭiyē. ulakanīti, valimaiyil tāṉ kaṭṭappaṭṭiruk kiṉṟatu. ulakattil aṭimaikaḷ aṭimaikaḷallātār eṉa iraṇṭu pirivukaḷ irunta oru kālamuṇṭu. mika nākarikamaṭainta makkaḷ uḷḷattilum ituvē niyāyamāṉa oḻuṅkupōlat tōṉṟiṟṟu. āṉāl atu niyāya māṉataṉṟu. itupōlavē peṇkaḷai aṭimaippaṭuttuvatu ulaka vaḻakka mākaviruntatu. peṇkaḷ aṭimaippaṭuttappaṭāmal iruppārkaḷēyāṉāl, atu iyaṟkaikku māṟupaṭṭatu eṉat tōṉṟalām. peṇkaḷ valimaiyi ṉāl aṭakkappaṭṭirukkavillai. peṇkaḷē tām aṭaṅkiyirukka virumpukiṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷ ataippaṟṟi veṟuppuk koḷḷavillai. avarkaḷ ataṟku iṇaṅki oḻukupavarkaḷāyirukkiṉṟaṉar. ākavē peṇkaḷ, āṇkaḷukku aṭaṅki naṭattal tāṉ iyaṟkai vitiyeṉap palar kūṟalām. uṇmai avvāṟaṉṟu. peṇkaḷiṉ oḻukkam āṇkaḷukku muṟṟum māṟupaṭṭatu eṉṟu koḷḷum muṟaiyil, ellāp peṇkaḷum iḷamai mutal payiṟṟappaṭukiṉṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷukkuc cuyaputti, cuya atikāram, cuya pātukāppu illaiyeṉṟum, avarkaḷ piṟarukku aṭaṅki naṭakka vēṇṭumeṉavum uḷḷattiṟ koḷḷumpaṭi paḻakkappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. ellā nīti nūlkaḷum, ellā uṇarccikaḷum, avarkaḷ maṟṟavarkaḷiṭat til taṅki vāḻavēṇṭum eṉṉum karuttaiyē vaṟpuṟuttukiṉṟaṉa.

peṇkaḷai aṟiyāmaiyil amiḻntumpaṭi ceytu, avarkaḷai aṭimaikaḷāka irukkumpaṭi āṭavar ceytārkaḷēyaṉṟip peṇkaḷ āṇ kaḷukku aṭaṅkivāḻa iṇaṅkukiṟārkaḷ eṉak kūṟutal uṇmaiyākat tōṉṟavillai. peṇkaḷukku viṭutalai vēṇṭum; peṇkaḷukku āṭciyiṟ paṅku vēṇṭum; aracāṅka capaikaḷukku uṟuppiṉarait teriva taṟkup peṇkaḷ tamatu viruppait terivikkum (ōṭṭu) urimai vēṇṭum eṉṉum kiḷarccikaḷ uṇṭāyirunta pōtellām, ataṉai ātarittup perun tokaiyāṉa peṇkaḷ tiraṇṭārkaḷ. itaṉāl peṇkaḷiṭattil āṇkaḷukku aṭaṅki naṭakka vēṇṭumeṉṉum iyalpāṉa maṉappāṉmai illaiyeṉṟum āṭavar, avarkaḷ aṭaṅki naṭakkumpaṭi, camayam eṉṟum, nīti eṉṟum peyar koṭuttup pala kayiṟukaḷāl avarkaḷaik kaṭṭi vaittuḷḷārkaḷ eṉṟum namakku naṉku viḷaṅkukiṉṟataṉṟō?

intiya nākarikattiṟ peṇkaḷ nilai
irukku vētattiṉ carvāṉukkira maṇikaiyil irupatu peṇ pulavarkaḷ varaiyiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷilum pala peṇ pulavarkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. cāḷukkiyar āṭciyil (ki.pi.980-1160) pala peṇ āṭcit talaivarkaḷum (Governors) atikārikaḷum iruntārkaḷ. ak kālattil peṇkaḷ irāṇuvap payiṟci peṟṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷ aracariṉ meykāppāḷarāka iruntu vantaṉar. avarkaḷ villaip payaṉpaṭutta naṉku payiṟci peṟṟiruntaṉar. araca kuṭumpap peṇkaḷ āpattuk kālaṅkaḷil āyutantāṅki vīṭukaḷaik kāttārkaḷ eṉṟu mattiya kālat teṉṉintiyap paṭṭaiyaṅkaḷ oṉṟiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. kaṉṉaṭap peṇkaḷ im muṟaiyil ciṟantu viḷaṅkiṉar. maicūril cittaṅkāli eṉṉum iṭattil ōr vīrappeṇ kirāmattil nikaḻnta pōril ki.pi.1041-il māṇṭāḷ. 1264-il vīrap peṇ orutti, koḷḷaikkāraṉai maṭakki aṭittataṟkāka mūkku aṇi oṉṟu vīrap patakkamākap peṟṟāḷ. nilkoṇṭa eṉṉum iṭattil kāṇappaṭṭa paṭṭaiyamoṉṟil oru ciṟṟaracukkuriya irāṇi, paṭai oṉṟai naṭattic ceṉṟa nikaḻcci kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. avaḷ 144il taṉatu tantaiyaik koṉṟa pakaivaraip paḻi vāṅkum poruṭṭup purinta pōril māṇṭāḷ. irācaputtira kuṭumpap peṇkaḷ vāḷum, īṭṭiyum payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. avarkaḷ paṭaiyai naṭattic cellakkūṭiyavarkaḷākavum, vēṇṭiya pōtu āṭciyai naṭattakkūṭiyavarkaḷākavum iruntaṉar. kūrmatēvi eṉṉum araci, taṉṉuṭaiya kaṇavaṉ iṟantapiṉ irācciyattai naṭatti, kutām uttiṉ eṉpavaṉiṉ paṭaiyaic citaṟa aṭittāḷ. yavākihir tēvi eṉpavaḷ taṉatu kaṇavaṉiṉ maraṇattukkuppiṉ pōr muṉaiyiṉ muṉṉaṇiyil niṉṟu pōrceytu maṭintāḷ. irācaputtirar varalāṟṟil ivvakai nikaḻccikaḷ pala kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

peṇkaḷukku irāṇuvap payiṟci aḷikkum vaḻakkam marāṭṭiya araca kuṭumpaṅkaḷilum iruntu vantatu. paṭaiyai naṭattuvataṟkuk kaṇavaṉ allatu putalvaṉ illātapōtu, araciyē paṭaiyai naṭattic ceṉṟāḷ. kōlāppūr camastāṉattait tāpitta tātāpāy eṉpavaḷ, āṭci naṭattiyatōṭu tāṉē paṭaiyaiyum naṭattic ceṉṟāḷ. ivaḷukku ilakkumipāy eṉpavaḷ paṭait talaimai vakittut taṉatu vīrattaik kāṇpittāḷ.

puttar kāla ilakkiyaṅkaḷil, peṇkaḷ nūl nūṟṟal, necavu ceytal, kampaḷi neytal, tuṇikkuc cāyamūṭṭutal mutaliya toḻilkaḷāl varuvāyai uṇṭupaṇṇik kaṇavariṉ vaṟumaiyaip pōkkiṉārkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. nūl nūṟṟal peṇkaḷiṉ toḻilāka nīṇṭakālam iruntu vantatu.
peṇkaḷum maṟumaṇamum

uyirp paṭaippukkaḷiṉ nōkkam cantatikaḷaip perukkuvatu eṉa, muṉ ōr iṭattiṟ kūṟiyuḷḷōm. uyirkaḷil āṇ peṇ eṉṉum vēṟupāṭukaḷum cantatiyaip perukkuvataṟkākavē uṇṭākiyiruk kiṉṟaṉa. cila uyirkaḷil āṇ, peṇ cērkkaiyiṉ piṉ maṭintu viṭu kiṉṟatu. itaṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭut tēṉī. uyirkaḷ cantatiyaip perukku vatākiya iyaṟkai vitikku māṟāka eḻuntatē vitavaikaḷiṉ vivāka maṟuppākum. icceyaṟkaik kaṭṭuppāṭu iyaṟkai vitikku māṟupaṭṭatu. intiya nāṭṭil vitavaikaḷ koṭumai aṭaintu varuvatupōla, vēṟenta nāṭṭilum aṭaintatillai.

vitavaikaḷ uṭaṉkaṭṭai ēṟavēṇṭum, allatu vāḻnāḷ muḻuvatum piramaccāraṇi virataṅkākka vēṇṭumeṉa, intiyariṉ oru cārāruṭaiya camaya nūlkaḷil eḻuti vaikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. piramacāriṇiyaip pōla, avaḷ veṟṟilai pōṭṭuk koḷḷakkūṭātu; veṇkala vaṭṭilil uṇavu koḷḷakkūṭātu. avaḷ oru nāḷil oru muṟai māttiram uṇṇa vēṇṭum; iraṇṭumuṟai uṇṇutalākātu; kaṭṭilil paṭuttal ākātu; vācaṉaip poruḷkaḷaip payaṉpaṭuttal ākātu. avaḷ tiṉamum iṟanta kaṇavaṉukku amutupaṭaittal vēṇṭum. ivvakaiyāṉa koṭiya caṭṭaṅkaḷ vēta kālattil irukkavillai. kaṇavaṉai iḻanta peṇ, kaṇavaṉiṉ cakōtaraṉai maṇantāḷ. itu ak kālattil potu vaḻakkāka viruntatu. tivārā eṉpataṟku iraṇṭāvatu kaṇavaṉ eṉṟu poruḷ. ic col peṇṇiṉ koḻunaṉaik kuṟikkiṉṟatu. piṇattaik koḷuttuñ camayattil iṟantavaṉiṉ cakōtaraṉ koḻuntiyiṉ kaiyaip piṭittu avaḷait taṉatu maṉaiviyākumpaṭi kēṭṭāṉ. iṟantavaṉiṉ maṉaivi, avaṉ cakōtaraṉai cērtalaippaṟṟi vētaṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu. taittiriya caṅkitai oru maṉaivi, orē kālattil iraṇṭu kaṇavarai vaittiruttal takutiyaṉṟu eṉak kūṟukiṉṟatu. ikkaruttu aitarēyap pirāmaṇattilum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. māpāratattil peṇkaḷiṉ maṟumaṇattait taṭukkum cila caṭṭaṅkaḷ eḻuntaṉa eṉṟu terikiṟatu; āṉāl vēta kālattil peṇ kaḷukku maṟumaṇameṉpatu carva cātāraṇamāṉatu. māpāratattiṉ cānti paruvattil peṇkaḷukku maṟumaṇañ ceytal iyalpu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu. yākñavalki, yārukkum contamallātirukkum peṇṇai (vitavaiyai) maṇam muṭikkumpaṭi kūṟukiṉṟār. itikāca kālattil, maṇam ceytu koḷḷappaṭum peṇ, kaṉṉiyāka irukka vēṇṭumeṉṉum kaṭṭāyam irukkavillai. kiruṭṭiṇaṉ, kollappaṭṭa narakaṉiṉ peṇ kaḷaic cērntāṉ. tiricaṅku, tāṉ koṉṟa vitarppa aracaṉiṉ maṉaiviyai maṇantu iraṇṭu putalvarkaḷaip peṟṟāṉ. tamayanti iṉṉoruvaṉiṉ maṉaiviyāka viruntāḷ eṉpatai aṟintum, irutupaṉṉaṉ avaḷai maṇakka virumpiṉāṉ. itikāca kālattil maṟumaṇaṅkaḷ putumaiyāṉavai yalla. paḻaiya nūlkaḷ, paḻam pōkkuṭaiya purōkitarāl tiruttappaṭṭa piṉpum, vitavaikaḷiṉ maṇattaik kuṟippiṭum nikaḻccikaḷ kāṇappaṭutal viyappukkuriyatē.

vētakāla muṭivil peṇkaḷiṉ maṟumaṇam iḻivākak karutappaṭa lāyiṟṟu. orumuṟai maṇañceytavaḷai maṇakkum oruvaṉ pāviyāvāṉ eṉa āpattampar kūṟiyuḷḷār. āpattampar kālattum vitavaikaḷiṉ maṇattait taṭuppataṟku ettaṉaṅkaḷ naṭantuvantaṉa veṉṟum terikiṉ ṟatu. vitavai vayiṟṟil pirāmaṇaṉ mūlam piṟappavaṉ pirāmaṇaṉ āvāṉ eṉṟum, avaṉ vāṇikam ceytu vāḻum pirāmaṇaṉai oppat tāḻntava ṉākak karutappaṭuvāṉeṉṟum maṉu kūṟiyuḷḷār. maṟumaṇam mūlam vitavai vayiṟṟil piṟantavaṉukkut tantaiyiṉ cottil nāliloṉṟukku urimaiyuṇṭu eṉak kautamar kūṟiyuḷḷār. vaciṭṭarum, viṭṭuṇuvum vitavai vayiṟṟil maṟumaṇattāṟ piṟakkum piḷḷaikaḷ vaḷarppup piḷḷai kaḷukkuc camamāṉavarkaḷ eṉak kūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ. oru peṇ valitil kavarappaṭṭuk kiriyaikaḷ mūlam maṇañceytu koṭukkappaṭāviṭṭāl, avaḷ iṉṉoruvaṉukkuk koṭukkappaṭalāmeṉṟu vaciṭṭar kūṟi yuḷḷār. kaṇavaṉirukkumpōtu, peṇ kiriyaimūlam māttiram maṇañ ceyyappaṭṭiruntu uṇmaiyāṉa maṇam (āṇ, peṇ cērkkai) naṭavā viṭṭāl, avaḷ maṟupaṭiyum maṇakkappaṭalām eṉa āpattampar kūṟi yuḷḷār. ayalūrukkuc ceṉṟa kaṇavaṉ, aintu āṇṭukaḷ varaiyum tirumpi varāviṭiṉ, avaḷ vēṟu kaṇavaṉai maṇakkalām; kaṇavaṉ iṟantapiṉ pirāmaṇa vakuppiṉaḷ aintu āṇṭukaḷ kaḻittum vaiciyap peṇ nāṉku āṇṭukaḷukkup piṉṉum, cūttirap peṇ mūṉṟu āṇṭukaḷ kaḻintum, maṇakkalām eṉa avarē kūṟiyuḷḷār. itaṉāl kaṇavaṉ iṟanta peṇkaḷ māttiramallar; kaṇavaṉai iḻakkāta peṇkaḷum maṟumaṇam ceyyalām eṉṉum caṭṭaṅkaḷ iruntaṉaveṉṟu terikiṟatu. kaliyāṇa nīkkam “mōkṣam” eṉappaṭṭatu. nāratar collum caṭṭam varumāṟu:

“kaṇavaṉ veḷināṭṭukkuc ceṉṟu tirumpāviṭil, pirāmaṇap peṇ eṭṭu āṇṭukaḷ kāttirukkavēṇṭum. avaḷukkup piḷḷai illāviṭṭāl nāṉku āṇṭukaḷ poṟuttirukkalām. piḷḷaiyuḷḷa cattiriyap peṇ nāṉku āṇṭukaḷ kāttirukkalām. ataṉpiṉ avaḷ iṉṉoruvaṉai maṇakkalām. piḷḷaiyuḷḷa vaiciyap peṇ nāṉku āṇṭukaḷum, piḷḷaiyillātavaḷ iraṇṭu āṇṭukaḷum poṟut tirukkalām. cūttirap peṇ kāttirukka vēṇṭiyatillai. itu piracāpati yiṉ caṭṭam.” ik kāraṇaṅkaḷāl maṟumaṇam kuṟṟamākātu.


nāratar kūṟuvatu: “peṇ, cantatiyaip perukkuvataṟkākap paṭaikkap paṭṭavaḷ. peṇvayal; āṇ vitaiyai vitaippavaṉ. vitaiyillāta vaṉukku vayalaik koṭukkakkūṭātu. vitaiyillātavaṉ vayalai āḷuva taṟkut takutiyaṟṟavaṉ. kaṇavaṉiṟantapiṉ taṉ koḻunaṉai alaṭciyañ ceytu ayalāṉaic cērpavaḷ keṭṭavaḷāvāḷ.”
